## Deterministic Cycle layout feasibility — 2026-10-25

ADV-02.1.3.2 progresses through [ADR0028](../architecture/decisions/0028-cycle-layout-feasibility.md), [pure solver](../../scripts/research/cycle-layout.mjs) and [protocol](../testing/smartart-cycle-layout.md). The exact cycle2 content profile receives owned equal-circle packing, deterministic semantic root order, explicit directed closure and size/spacing/start/direction parameters. Empty/singleton policies are distinct. Two nodes use separate analytically clipped circular arcs rather than coincident reversed chords; three or more use explicit directed block-arrow polygons. Full primitive bounds determine fit; minimum/explicit diameters reject when unavailable. Frame rotation/mirroring and upright text placement are explicit, without native cache generation or public package imports.

Text uses an 80%-scaled inscribed square. No provider means located unmeasured findings. An explicit synchronous callback supplies bounds, including negative bearings; the solver records raw metrics, text origin and placed bounds. Fit checks use the centered actual extent; strict overflow throws. Initial real-font integration exposed negative top bearing y=-1 for short pinned-font labels, so explicit placement was implemented rather than discarding the origin or widening tolerance. Long labels retain overflow. Wrappers around public resolveLayout must propagate missing-measurement diagnostics: unsupported glyphs and disposed providers cannot become zero-size success.

Six pure groups cover hand-derived square/triangle centers/arrows, zero/one/two-node behavior, storage independence and semantic order, start/direction/mirrors/rotation, text measurement/negative bearings/provider failures, invalid graphs/options, exact work/text/geometry budgets and retry. The seeded oracle now uses high random bits to avoid low-bit flip/direction correlations; 120 diagrams vary frame/angle/gap/transform. Pairwise circle separation, every block-arrow segment, two-node arc samples/head segments and inverse-frame containment are independently checked. Two browser groups connect the existing public loaded-font measurement adapter with pinned Roboto Mono and explicit ASCII coverage, reject unavailable CJK/disposal, and compare actual SVG circles/path bounds with hand expectations under a 0.001-point browser observation tolerance. [Manual preview review](../testing/smartart-cycle-layout-review.json) hashes the inspected two-/four-node image and separates visible findings from automated proof.

The [optional native-box comparison](../../scripts/inspect-smartart-cycle-layout.mjs) checks six retained PowerPoint stages with 3/4/5 nodes. It verifies file hashes and explicit presentation/content/cache model associations; Python ZIP/XML/Decimal extracts only direct untransformed native circle boxes and frame coordinates. A separate Python packing calculation verifies proposed boxes and every delta. [Evidence](../testing/smartart-cycle-layout-evidence.json) retains maximum coordinate differences of 0.2354969366 points for the three-node cases, 0.1366368720 for four and 0.1644790650 for five. **All six fail the explicit 0.1-point research threshold.** These observations cannot close native layout admission; native arrow shapes, text/style fitting, cache freshness, two-node native behavior and other parameters are not compared. LibreOffice ellipse observations remain separately recorded. No new native UI interaction was performed.

Adversarial review checked five failure paths: (1) near-native coordinates masquerading as compatibility, prevented by retained failed thresholds and separate native admission; (2) two reversed edges overlapping, prevented by explicit clipped half-orbits plus browser/manual/sample checks; (3) negative glyph bearings being dropped or short text falsely failing, fixed with recorded placement and loaded-font tests; (4) mirror/rotation/angle extrema escaping fit, checked through actual primitive bounds and independent inverse/sample oracles; (5) unmeasured/missing-font/long text becoming silent success or budgets resetting, prevented by located findings, provider failure propagation, strict overflow and exact work boundaries. Paint-stroke/effect extents are outside the centerline/fill contract and remain explicit.

The full objective remains active. Next work remains native gap/rounding/paint attribution, more frame/direction/font parameters, native two-node behavior, heterogeneous sizes, full rich text and cache policies; Process/Hierarchy layouts and public family admission remain open. No parent TODO is closed by this private experiment.


Final validation — 2026-10-26: `pnpm build`, `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm lint`, `pnpm test` and `git diff --check` passed for this slice. The full suite includes 32 diagram research groups and 67 import groups, together with all existing package, visual, browser, release and installed-consumer checks. The final expanded high-bit-seeded/arc-sampling version also passed six pure layout groups. Eight targeted pure/browser groups passed, including loaded-font/negative-bearing/provider-error behavior. The six native comparisons completed their independent checks while retaining all six declared-threshold mismatches; they are not compatibility passes. The reviewed PNG hash remains unchanged after the full run. Logs: `.tmp/cycle-layout-{build,typecheck,lint,full-test,focused-final,adversarial,native-comparison-final}.log`.


## Cache dependency/publication protocol and Cycle attribution — 2026-10-26

[ADR0029](../architecture/decisions/0029-diagram-cache-consistency-research.md) advances ADV-03.7.1/.7.2 and ADV-02.1.3.3 through a [private cache session](../../scripts/research/diagram-cache-session.mjs), [spacing observation](../../scripts/research/cycle-spacing-observation.mjs) and [protocol](../testing/smartart-cache-consistency.md). No production package imports them and public preview/preservation behavior stays unchanged. A managed witness is scoped to explicit supplied dependency descriptors and generator/validator versions; imported bytes remain unknown and complete dependency coverage is still caller-provided evidence, not a proof derived by this module.

The cache session captures exact owner/layout, component/theme/resource SHA-256 descriptors, frame size/transforms and environment/generator keys. Sorted dependency-set order is stable; other changes invalidate conservatively. Source missing/empty/unknown, invalidated and managed-current states are separate. Explicit block/omit/rebuild policies never silently reuse source bytes. Generation and independent validation stage detached byte plans; commit checks session ownership, revision, publication epoch, signal and single use. Input changes, ABA, other publications, cancellation, disposal, mutation at validation completion and failures cannot replace the current selection. Read returns copies. Disposal immediately detaches the external signal relay and clears session-held plans/buffers. Cancellation remains cooperative with callbacks; this is not a native/package/file transaction or OPC deletion authority.

Seven pure cache groups cover every dependency category, explicit input restoration, staged commit, exact snapshot/payload quotas, actual Uint8Array byte-length checks, callback/validation failure and mutation, async generation/validation races, cross-session/forged/consumed/released/stale plans, successful/dispose-time listener cleanup, and plan quotas. A 120-round high-bit seeded state oracle independently checks update, policy, release, failure, stale publication and commit outcomes. Snapshot byte accounting now charges escaped JSON/UTF-8 per descriptor before concatenating the whole snapshot, including a control-character amplification negative case. One browser integration generates an actual owned Cycle geometry-only SVG, independently checks metadata, circle boxes and directed polygon structure, rejects old bytes for changed data/colors, aborts stale work before validation, validates a replacement, and proves serialized bytes do not carry a managed witness into a new session. This SVG is not inserted into DiagramML and does not claim text/native fidelity.

Four pure spacing groups test a hand-derived ring, nearest-EMU residuals, bad ordering/non-ring/corrupt observations, exact work limits and retry. The optional [native comparison runner](../../scripts/inspect-smartart-cycle-layout.mjs) now independently extracts stroke width/alignment and reconstructs an ordered uniform-circle ring. Native gap ratios are approximately 0.5042376891 (three nodes), 0.5016993917 (four), and 0.5031979711 (five); maximum ring reconstruction residuals are below one EMU. Nearest-EMU rounding of the proposed geometry still leaves maximum deltas around 0.1366–0.2355 points, over the unchanged 0.1-point research threshold. A theme-change pair changes explicit stroke width 1.5 → 1 point while exact circle boxes remain identical. Python independently verifies both ring inference and rounding calculations. Observed parameters remain descriptive; the deterministic solver is not calibrated with inferred constants and no universal stroke/rounding fix is asserted.

Adversarial review addressed five likely failures: (1) imported or serialized cache bytes masquerading as current, prevented by session-owned witnesses and actual DOM dependency rejection; (2) input ABA or another publication admitting stale output, prevented by separate revision/epoch and callback/ready-plan race tests; (3) validators mutating the inspected candidate or spoofed byteLength bypassing payload bounds, covered by private copies/native byte access and negative tests; (4) abandoned plans, ignored cancellation or abort listeners retaining resources without stated ownership, bounded by explicit plan quotas/release, one running generator and immediate dispose cleanup, with cooperative callback semantics documented; (5) fitting observed constants to pass native layout comparison, prevented by unchanged failed thresholds, independent quantum/stroke controls and descriptive-only inferred ratios.

Full source dependency derivation, genuine stale native-cache application fixtures, accepted native layout/font/paint policies, native cache generation, whole-package atomic updates and public APIs remain open. Existing parent TODOs and the overall goal remain incomplete.


Validation for this slice: `pnpm build`, `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm lint`, `pnpm test` and `git diff --check` passed. The final full run has 43 diagram research groups and 68 import groups plus all existing package, visual, browser, release and installed-consumer checks. Twelve targeted cache/spacing/browser groups passed; the final explicit forged-plan negative case also passes in the seven-group cache suite. Native spacing attribution independently checked all six retained stages, preserving every existing 0.1-point threshold failure. ## Direct diagram text observations and controlled cache disagreement — 2026-10-26

[ADR0030](../architecture/decisions/0030-diagram-text-observations.md) advances ADV-03.7.3 with an explicit public inspection slice: `diagramTextComparison: "direct-paragraphs"` adds exact paragraph-vector observations over associated data/cache pairs. Paragraph boundaries, soft breaks, empty text, XML-decoded CR, whitespace and Unicode are exact. Equal/different observations retain unknown freshness, do not certify semantic ownership/visual equality, and add no native edit or source-write authority. Scalar text and repeated comparisons share cumulative diagram work/finding limits. Invocation-time option capture extends through Node files and actual candidate validation.

Seven focused groups cover both dialects, exact characters, ambiguity/noncontent references, missing/opaque/field text, partial/MCE graphs, shared owners/cache pairs, cumulative character/finding limits, option capture, abort, exact no-op and unrelated text save. Three integration groups compare Node/browser ESM/global reports against independent DOM-derived text/raw indices, retain all non-slide payloads across three unrelated source-save rounds, and verify file-import option capture. Installed Node and readonly declaration consumers exercise the public type/flag. All ten targeted groups pass. The initial real-file runner failed before native conversion because Core shape text can use a string as well as paragraph arrays; its test-only extractor now handles both forms.

The optional [controlled runner](../../scripts/inspect-smartart-stale-cache.mjs) creates three families × five variants from immutable hash-pinned PowerPoint outputs: baseline, data-only, cache-only, both and empty cache. Python independently verifies changed-part sets and exact unmodified payloads; Parser checks selected text vectors, exact no-op and stored-cache preview. [Retained evidence](../testing/smartart-stale-cache-evidence.json) includes fifteen LibreOfficeDev 26.8.0.0.alpha0 save/reopen cases (45 conversion operations), independently inspected source/native ZIP/XML and direct/reopened PDF characters. All complete. Across each selected family, data-only markers survive in data while PDFs still show the original cache label; cache-only markers appear in PDFs while source data remains original. Both-side markers appear in PDFs. Empty controls display the original label but still serialize zero DSP cache shapes, so rendering is not proof of regenerated stored cache. PowerPoint stale-cache UI behavior, geometry/style/font freshness, rich-text/layout roles and family-wide cache policies remain untested. Third-party artifacts remain local pending redistribution admission.

Adversarial review addressed five failure paths: (1) equal strings being promoted to freshness, prevented by literal unknown across every pair/preview and explicit contract; (2) labels/raw traversal positions being guessed as ownership, prevented by explicit model evidence, per-part identities and independent DOM checks; (3) fields/opaque/ambiguous source text being flattened into false equality, prevented by unavailable/source-only controls; (4) long/shared text bypassing bounded work or differing findings, prevented by aggregate per-character/per-pair charges and negative budget tests; (5) an inspection option accidentally granting cached edits or corrupting unrelated payloads, prevented by unchanged unbound-save guards, option capture/candidate validation and three-round byte checks. Native observations are kept distinct from public layout/write admission. No parent TODO or overall implementation goal is closed.


Validation: `pnpm build`, `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm lint`, `pnpm test` and `git diff --check` passed. The full suite includes Parser 556, diagram research 43 and import 71 groups, plus all existing Core/Layout/Exporter/SVG/visual/browser/release/installed-consumer checks. All ten targeted text groups pass. A separate retained-result audit checks fifteen source/control hashes and each direct/reopened/native data/cache marker result; every original remains unchanged. Logs: `.tmp/diagram-text-{build,typecheck,lint,parity,full-test}.log`, `.tmp/stale-cache-native.log`. The controlled native outputs are retained in the manifest's unique local run directory.


## PowerPoint cache-conflict matrix and repeated rebuilds — 2026-10-26

[ADR0031](../architecture/decisions/0031-native-stale-cache-policy.md) advances ADV-03.7.3 and ADV-02.1.3.3 with actual PowerPoint 16.104 build 16.104.25121423 GUI operations. The preceding direct-text slice changed authoritative code/tests/evidence, so the previous goal turn is classified as progress. This continuation uses immutable hash-pinned controlled inputs and separate native copies. No delete action was performed or prior one-time delete consent reused.

All fifteen controls (three layouts × baseline/data-only/cache-only/both/empty) were opened, saved with Command-S, closed and reopened through the native UI. Initial/reopened screenshots and AX are retained. PowerPoint shows source data markers in data-only/both cases, original data labels in cache-only/empty cases, and writes regenerated matching cache characters, including eight stored shapes for Process/Hierarchy and six for Cycle after empty-cache saves. No repair dialog was observed. The named LibreOffice version instead displays/reuses the nonempty stored cache and keeps conflicts. This is evidence for those specimens/versions; full native fidelity is not inferred.

Three independent data-only copies receive actual native layout change/return and save/reopen: Process 重点流程 → 基本流程 → 重点流程; Hierarchy 标准 → 左悬挂 → 标准; Cycle 基本循环 → 块循环 → 基本循环. Intermediate/restored/reopened images retain source markers and native SmartArt design controls. Independent ZIP/XML confirms retained content identities, roles, paragraphs and ordered parent edges; presentation identity/parameter continuity is not promised. Long marker strings visibly trigger native text fitting and different sizes, so these images are not visual-parity passes.

Six further data-only controls extend the original data-only save into three consecutive native rebuild rounds per family. Each mutation starts from the previous native saved output, changes one selected content point's text in the data part and leaves cached text old. The independent verifier proves only the data payload changes, a real pre-open cache disagreement exists, and the previous-source linkage hashes match. All new source markers are visible on open/reopen; each native save repairs directly comparable source/cache characters while content IDs/roles/order remain stable. These controlled file mutations followed by native rebuilds are not native UI text-edit cycles. Process/Hierarchy retain 46 parts/43 relationships/5 diagram-directory parts/zero media; Cycle retains 26/23/5/zero. Three-round ZIP byte sizes are 43,590/43,589/43,591; 45,818/45,825/45,824; 19,312/19,312/19,313. No arbitrary long-run growth claim is made.

The [retained-output verifier](../../scripts/inspect-smartart-stale-cache-powerpoint.mjs) checks **24** outputs with public Parser inspection, opt-in text reports, exact no-op and an independent Python ZIP/ElementTree content/cache reconstruction. It hashes source, native snapshots and all image/AX artifacts, distinguishes copied saved bytes from reopened files, checks all content identities/roles/paragraphs/ordered parent edges and retains changed-part inventories and counts. The first attempt incorrectly asserted equal vectors for intentionally conflicting inputs; that test logic now checks equality only for native outputs and explicitly requires repeated-round input disagreement. The initial UI file-path helper also needed a full AX snapshot because diff lines carry `+` prefixes; no file was opened/saved under a guessed path after that guard rejected.

The [adversarial runner](../../scripts/test-smartart-stale-cache-powerpoint.mjs) verifies three independent failures in separate retained copies: saved/reopened byte mismatch; cache text corruption; consistent content-GUID replacement with valid references/equal characters. Each rejects for its intended reason and produces no successful evidence file. Original native and positive evidence hashes remain exact. This closes the risk that cache text equality alone could be mistaken for stable content identity. [Positive evidence](../testing/smartart-stale-cache-powerpoint-evidence.json), [negative evidence](../testing/smartart-stale-cache-powerpoint-negative-evidence.json) and [protocol](../testing/smartart-stale-cache.md) record scope and reproduction. Native binary artifacts stay local pending redistribution admission.

Adversarial review addressed five likely failures: (1) extrapolating one producer's regeneration to all consumers, prevented by the conflicting named PowerPoint/LibreOffice matrix; (2) calling a cached image or successful no-op proof of native identity, prevented by actual GUI layout changes and independent content-GUID negative controls; (3) repeating one original file and calling it three rounds, prevented by prior-output hashes and exact sole-data-part mutation checks; (4) hiding new parts/media/relationship leaks, prevented by source/native inventories and observed stable round counts; (5) interpreting equal native output text as complete freshness or permission to edit source, prevented by unchanged unknown freshness and the explicit synchronized-cache/public-admission boundary in ADR0031.

Validation: the 24-case positive verifier and three-case negative runner pass; 43 diagram research and 10 focused text/DOM/browser/file/preservation groups pass; `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm lint` (including boundaries, local links and typed examples) and `git diff --check` pass. Engine source is unchanged in this continuation; the preceding full build/full suite remains applicable, and no unsupported complete-test claim is made for native editing/playback. Logs: `.tmp/stale-cache-powerpoint-{verifier-final,negatives,typecheck,lint,research-tests,text-tests}.log`. Full source dependency derivation, shared/image/rich-text variants, accepted geometry/style/font layout/cache generation, native node-edit matrix and whole-package publication remain open. Parent TODOs and the full requested implementation goal remain incomplete.


## Read-only inspect/compare CLI — 2026-10-26

[ADR0032](../architecture/decisions/0032-read-only-cli-inspection.md) advances ECO-09.1.1/.1.2 from bootstrap output to actual installed `pptkit inspect` / `compare` commands and public `runCliAsync`. The preceding PowerPoint matrix changed authoritative native evidence and policy, so the previous goal turn is classified as progress. This slice makes existing public Parser inspection/import capability reports and exact/conservative-XML OPC comparison usable from local command workflows. It adds no parsing/package-write implementation and does not claim native verification or public SmartArt authoring.

CLI owns strict argument parsing, captured cwd/signal, regular-file reads, bounded transport and process orchestration in separate modules. Unknown/repeated/missing/cross-command options reject before I/O. The existing synchronous `runCli` help use remains supported; file commands use the async entry. Local files open nonblocking, reject directories/FIFOs/devices/sockets, check size before allocation, read bounded chunks and check EOF/size/mtime/ctime. Symlinks retain normalized relative argument paths; input metadata reports captured byte length/SHA-256. External resources are not fetched. Inputs are sequential and per-file bounded; Parser expansion/work limits remain in force.

The versioned JSON envelope includes selected command/status, relative input identities, nativeVerification not-performed and the exact public report. Inspect supports structure/content and explicit cache/direct-text/text-save inspection profiles, disposes its session and reports no write permission beyond existing findings. Human output includes located feature/operation/tier/reason records and diagnostics; absent findings cannot imply support. Compare defaults to exact payloads, requires opt-in XML normalization and retains the public difference-count/truncation contract. Difference reports use stdout/exit 1; error envelopes use stderr and stable usage/input/limit/internal/cancellation codes. JSON encoding charges escaped UTF-8 tokens/final newline incrementally; output exhaustion emits a valid bounded error rather than a partial successful report. Human control/directional formatting characters are escaped. SIGINT forwards cooperative cancellation and output EPIPE does not expose a stack.

CLI now depends on public Parser; its TypeScript reference and workspace lock entry are updated, with no new external package. Architecture/package/release-order docs reflect Parser before CLI. [API contract](../api/cli-inspection.md), typed example, package README, roadmap and original task status are updated. Full task/evidence-linked capability catalogs, mixed native matrices, advanced Parser budget profiles and authoring/source-save commands remain open; ECO-09 parents are not closed by this transport.

Ten CLI groups cover Strict/Transitional exact public-report parity; deterministic absolute/relative input transport and unchanged bytes; explicit cached-text conflict/save profiles; exact versus XML equality; retained-difference truncation; malformed packages and numeric/command/option rejection; bounded input/output errors/retry; invocation-time mutable args/options and cancellation; dash/Unicode/control names, symlinks and actual nonblocking FIFO rejection; real executable stdout/stderr/statuses; exact UTF-8 escaped JSON/human output/newline boundaries; and synchronous help. Isolated packed/npm-installed tests additionally execute the real shim for inspect, equal/truncated-different comparison and missing input; declaration consumers compile the async API, schema literal and readonly options.

Adversarial review addressed five likely failures: (1) truncated differences becoming false equality, prevented by separate total count/equal/retained findings and exit-1 tests; (2) budget overflow emitting incomplete JSON or silently omitting capabilities, prevented by bounded encoding and explicit limit-error tests including exact UTF-8/newline thresholds; (3) FIFO/large/changing inputs hanging or bypassing capture, addressed by nonblocking regular-file/stat/EOF checks, size-before-allocation and actual FIFO tests; (4) terminal filename controls injecting output or absolute OS errors leaking context, prevented by escaped human text, relative input labels and generic filesystem messages, with control/dash/Unicode fixtures; (5) CLI bypassing Parser authority, leaking sessions or introducing invalid dependencies, prevented by public API-only orchestration, immediate session disposal, exact public-report comparisons, boundary checks and real installed dependency/shim tests. Metadata/EOF guards are conservative local capture checks, not proof of an atomic filesystem-wide snapshot; no such claim is made.

Validation: `pnpm build`, `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm lint`, `pnpm test` and `git diff --check` pass. The full suite includes 10 CLI groups, Parser 556, diagram research 43 and import 71 groups, plus all existing Core/Layout/Exporter/SVG/visual/browser/release/installed-consumer checks. Ten targeted CLI groups also pass independently. Logs: `.tmp/cli-commands-{focused-final,build,typecheck,lint,full-test}.log`. No commit, publication or source deck modification was performed. The full requested implementation objective remains incomplete and active.


## Versioned seven-operation capability observations — 2026-10-26

[ADR0033](../architecture/decisions/0033-import-capability-observations.md) advances ECO-09.1.1/.1.2 and IMP-04.4 through a pure public `describeImportCapabilities` view and explicit CLI selection. The previous CLI turn changed production commands, tests and contracts and is classified as progress. This continuation makes exact source findings queryable without granting native/source-save authority or declaring the broad capability catalog complete.

Parser owns versioned readonly subject/assessment/reference/report types, default quotas and the bounded transformation. Subjects retain feature plus all part/path/raw object/relationship/element index and Core slide/element identities. Seven operation rows are always present; missing rows are unreported, tier disagreements conflicting, and only agreeing observed tiers receive a tier. Duplicate reasons/IDs remain observations. No part/property/operation inherits another subject's preservation or editability. Input reports are not authenticated, and both nativeVerification and candidateSaveValidation remain not-performed. The output covers reported findings, not every source object/property or binding/candidate condition.

A small versioned reference index adds actual repository task/artifact navigation for known structural/source/metadata/section/transition/drawing/chart/table/notes/SmartArt categories. Prefix rules remain category hints; they do not recognize new semantics or prove tests/native observations ran for this input. Unknown features remain without fabricated references. Source/feature and observation ordering use locale-independent code units. Future fields are ignored without invoking unknown getters; known fields require own data properties on plain records, dense findings and supported enums/raw indices. All findings, including filtered-out entries, consume cumulative input/text/work limits and remain validated. Shared-prefix comparisons also consume work. MaxSubjects alone truncates with complete retained subjects, all seven rows/observations and full matching counts/status totals; zero is counts-only. Output is detached/frozen and no session or source handle is retained.

`inspect --capabilities` explicitly selects the nested versioned report with reportKind capabilities; default inspect/compare remain compatible. Exact feature/part flags and complete-subject quotas require this option, reject on compare and retain existing output/error/cancellation boundaries. API/CLI examples, package/reference/architecture docs, roadmap and original TODO progress are updated. The current audit matrix's obsolete bootstrap/five-package/native-lock statements are corrected against current executable/package/native evidence.

Eight focused groups cover exact identity/separator collisions, missing/conflicting/repeated findings, 40 seeded shuffled passes checked against an independent operation oracle, complete-subject truncation/counts/filtering, additive fields/accessor rejection, invalid projections/enums/locations, exact finding/text/work bounds and detached retry, actual task/artifact links and real both-dialect cached-preview/no-op sessions. An additional negative case isolates long-reason sorting by comparing the counts-only work budget with a retained-subject query; malformed findings outside a filter still reject. CLI now has eleven groups including explicit views, zero-subject summaries and cross-command/flag failures. Browser integration reconstructs every recognized provenance field/observation independently, checks complete operations and exact no-op, and compares Node/ESM/global reports with network blocked. Its first JSON-string oracle incorrectly treated object key order as provenance; the fixed oracle compares individual source fields. Installed Node/shim/declaration consumers exercise the pure API, schema literal, complete operation vectors, truncation and readonly references.

Adversarial review addressed five likely failures: (1) missing findings or omitted subjects being interpreted as support, prevented by unreported states, coverage scope and full totals/truncation; (2) generic part preservation or an editable observation winning conflicts, prevented by exact tuple grouping, preserved duplicates and no tier on conflicts; (3) a linked native artifact turning into current-input certification, prevented by independent fixed not-performed states and reference-only contracts/tests; (4) filtering/shared long prefixes bypassing quotas, prevented by complete-input validation/accounting and isolated sorting-budget negatives; (5) source/authoring mutation or report forgery granting save authority, prevented by detached frozen observation values, known-accessor guards, unchanged session bindings/plans and real no-op plus broad source-save regression. Work units are implementation diagnostics and can vary with equivalent input ordering; only output row ordering is canonical.

Validation: `pnpm build`, `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm lint`, `pnpm test` and `git diff --check` pass. Full tests include Parser 564, CLI 11, import integration 72 and diagram research 43 groups plus existing Core/Layout/Exporter/SVG/visual/browser/release/installed consumers. The final expanded eight-group adversarial suite and independent browser parity pass separately. Logs: `.tmp/capabilities-{full-build,typecheck,lint,full-test,focused,adversarial-final,cli-tests,parity-final}.log`. Complete source-property inventory, conditional candidate-save readiness, authored capabilities, exact broad evidence/native certification and ECO-09/IMP-04 parent acceptance remain open. The full requested implementation goal remains incomplete and active.


## Existing chart numeric preservation saves — 2026-10-26

推进 IMP-09.2.6.1/.2 的有界切片，新增 [ADR0034](../architecture/decisions/0034-chart-numeric-preservation-save.md)、[公开契约与可检查示例](../api/chart-value-save.md)。显式 `chartEditSaveProfile: "existing-values-v1"` 与文字 profile 独立，可共同构造一份原子候选；不升级默认权限，不关闭图表源保存及继承父项。

实现与证据：

- 源绑定只接受完整 dense cache、明确本地 A1 的名称/类别/数值、完全一致且不共享的内嵌 XLSX；公开 `chartValues` 给出 Core 系列/点与 raw cache、workbook/sheet/cell 身份。负零按 Object.is 区分，缺失/空白不变成零。
- Parser 先暂存 chart `c:v` 和嵌套 worksheet `v` 的词法补丁，再重包 XLSX 与外层 PPTX；独立比较两层批准载荷，实际候选再投影/再绑定 cache 和 workbook 数值与身份，最后验证完整 Core 状态。会话基线不推进；错误、取消、过期无部分输出。
- [11 组 Parser 测试](../../packages/pptx-parser/test/chart-save.test.mjs)覆盖两方言、bar/line/pie 三轮、无关双层载荷与词法周边完全不变、数字拼写/负零/单元格格式、缺失冲突/公式/外链/auto-update、同关系多 frame、不同 chart 共用 workbook、标签别名、独立多图、混合文字/非法拓扑、无改/还原/取消/过期/预算/重试、第二层失败和故意错指等值单元格。
- [3 组跨运行时集成](../../scripts/chart-value-save-parity.test.mjs)覆盖 Node/ESM/global 三轮字节/报告一致，浏览器 DOM 独立读取 cache 与 worksheet 且禁网，文件入口调用时捕获，以及 Exporter 生成的三个家族多系列源文件三轮保存。安装后的 Node/声明消费者也覆盖新 profile 和只读 provenance。

交付前对抗式审查与处理：

1. **只写 cache 假同步**：注入原值相同但错误的 worksheet 目标；cache/Core 单独可对齐，实际再绑定仍拒绝。保留公有正确绑定可重试的对照。
2. **共享/别名误写**：检查 chart 入边、raw frame 实际使用次数、workbook 入边和所有已解释角色的 cell 使用；两图共享 XLSX、重复数值引用与数值兼标签均阻止。未知 chart 公式也阻止，未引入隐式复制/原位共享策略。
3. **嵌套资源预算逃逸与恰好耗尽**：内层解包保留调用方更紧的展开上限；内外比较共享 bytes/work，恰好耗尽先转为 `save-limit-exceeded`，不泄露无效限额参数错误。staged bytes 保守累计，取消与限额后均可重试。
4. **绑定查找扩张**：实际候选按精确 Core 四元身份建立索引，避免每个提案线性扫描全部绑定；循环保留取消检查。未知扩展原样保留，但不声称已证明其全部依赖语义。
5. **虚假原生准入与默认兼容**：默认不生成 chartValues/不授权编辑，文字 profiles 权限不变；原生 Edit Data、结构/格式写入、完整依赖策略仍未验证/未准入，文档与 TODO 保持开放。

验证：`pnpm build`、`pnpm typecheck`、`pnpm lint`、完整 `pnpm test` 均退出 0；完整轮次 Parser 574 项（当时 10 组新增 Parser 测试），最终补入故意错指测试并改为身份索引后，另跑 Parser **575/575**、typecheck/lint 全通过。最终定向 **14/14**；完整导入集成 **75/75**。完整套件还包括 Core 74、Layout 126、Exporter 49、SVG 19、CLI 11、dev-app 16、visual 27、timing 10、diagram research 43，以及浏览器/安装消费者等，均通过。`git diff --check` 无错误。过程日志位于本地 `.tmp/chart-values-*.log`；这些不是提交后的永久原生认证。

接续：更多原生产器/复杂 workbook 准入、完整隐式依赖和显式共享策略；IMP-09.2.6.3 单独处理系列/类别结构变更；.6.4 需具名应用实际 Edit Data、保存重开和三轮证据。当前测试不代替原生交互验收，所有相关父项保持未勾选。


## Independent and native chart numeric source-save evidence — 2026-10-26

推进 IMP-09.2.6.4 的独立检查/headless 切片，保持原任务与父项未勾选。[说明](../testing/chart-value-save.md)、[完整证据](../testing/chart-value-save-evidence.json)、[原生 runner](../../scripts/inspect-chart-value-save.mjs)已落盘。新增仓库自有四页样本：bar/line/pie 分页、第四张未改 bar 对照，bar/line 还有未改第二系列和各页 notes。

- 三轮均从上一轮 PPTKit 保存结果重新导入，以 `existing-values-v1` 改三个既有点。三轮分别为 `[12.5,13.5,14.5]`、`[0,0,0]`、`[37.25,38.25,39.25]`。45 个 outer parts 不变，仅 3 个 chart 与 3 个 XLSX 改动，其余 39 个载荷完全相同；所有 inner part 集合与未批准载荷不变，XML 属性/结构/非批准数值保持。
- 独立 Python 标准库 ZIP/XML/Decimal oracle 不读取 Parser 绑定/快照，沿 presentation→slide→chart→XLSX→worksheet 关系与源公式定位，独立核对 cache/单元格、身份/顺序、负零、两层差异。它是有界观测工具，只处理样本所需的单列 numeric A1 范围，不成为公共 SpreadsheetML 导入器。
- LibreOfficeDev **26.8.0.0.alpha0 / 2c87e51eeaa2b413ff4ae097b2705eea1995d8e5**, macOS arm64，分别重开/重存三份结果。Python 独立读回全部四图和所有系列值，与候选相等；最终候选 PDF 有四页及各页标题。
- 原生保存结果变为 48 个 parts，图表没有引用的内嵌 XLSX。每次导入仍可原字节 no-op；实改数字再 `prepareSave(edit)` 都返回 blocked / `save-edit-unsupported` / 无 plan。将 cache 保留和源保存资格分别留证，不放宽 profile，不以 native cache 数据保留冒充 Edit Data。

对抗式审查后处理六类风险：

1. **自证回环**：Python reader 不调用 PPTKit；除保存提案外，验证目标均由独立关系/公式读取定位。另有双方言、三家族三轮与四页/双系列对照。
2. **“只改 cache”或错误目标**：验证器拒绝陈旧 XLSX、源不一致、错误期望值、重复/非法提案、共享 workbook；数值必须在两层同身份同时吻合。
3. **夹带修改**：故意修改未选 chart、chart 属性、cell 样式、inner/outer 新增 part 均拒绝；多系列同 worksheet 的三项修改另验，避免只覆盖单提案路径。
4. **危险/歧义观察输入**：归档/展开/XML bytes、节点/深度有界，重复 ZIP 名、DTD/entity 和超深 XML 拒绝；非有限值与负索引明确失败。数值负零独立验正反例。
5. **原生权限假升级**：manifest 显式记录引用 XLSX 缺失、unmapped 状态、真实 numeric 保存阻止和 no-op 原字节结果。headless/PDF 结论与交互/外观/引用闸门分离。
6. **可复跑与环境耦合**：可选 `pnpm test:chart-save-evidence` 运行 Python 验证器测试；标准 `pnpm test` 不新增 Python/LibreOffice 依赖。原生程序和 PyMuPDF 仅可选 runner 使用，唯一临时目录/独立用户 profile 避免触碰用户文稿。

本轮 `pnpm build`、`pnpm typecheck`、`pnpm lint` 均通过；定向独立证据测试最终为六组，含变异拒绝；具名原生三轮实际运行成功。此前完整功能基线通过，本轮未修改包运行时代码，未重复整套功能测试。最终 `git diff --check` 与文档链接/TypeScript 示例检查通过。

原生 UI 工具实际返回 Mac locked / automatic unlock failed，已向用户发出解锁请求；没有通过其他控制渠道绕过，也没有伪造 PowerPoint 交互证据。PowerPoint/LibreOffice 实际 Edit Data、从已准入原生产器 XLSX 起步的三轮、格式/结构/隐式引用准入继续待完成，不因本轮 headless 证据关闭 IMP-09.2.6.4。


## Public detached finite timeline arithmetic — 2026-10-26

推进 ADV-06.1.1.1 的有限数值契约，交付 [ADR0035](../architecture/decisions/0035-public-finite-timeline-arithmetic.md)、[API/类型/预算/示例](../api/finite-timeline.md)。未将纯时间算术算作目标/效果创作、格式映射或播放准入；ADV-06/07 及父项依赖、分数/无限重复、autoReverse/end/restart 等仍保持开放。

实现：Core root/browser 公开 `compileFiniteTimeline`、`sampleFiniteTimeline` 和带 code/path 的 `FiniteTimelineError`。`span/sequence/parallel` 明确整数毫秒、父/子 delay、零时长、正整数 repeat、remove/hold、同刻边界顺序。输出冻结、带 `pptkit-finite-timeline-v1` 的 schedule，随机时刻采样只算抽象 progress。repeat 独立于 node 预算且不展开；算术先精确核对 safe integer，再检查时间上限。未新增 package/dependency，也不向 authoring/Canonical IR/Layout 加动画字段。

[9 组 Core 测试](../../packages/core/test/timeline.test.mjs)：

- 手算嵌套串并行/延迟时间戳及同刻 start/end 顺序；两种 fill、start/内部 repeat/final/zero 边界与乱序 seek。
- 单 span 的 MAX_SAFE_INTEGER repeat 和 near-safe-integer 算术，正例仍只有两个边界；加法/乘法溢出、各预算恰好通过/失败与重试。
- 输入快照/冻结、null-prototype 数据、负零时间归一；未知/autoReverse/分数/无限/事件目标等明确拒绝，不近似；访问器/自定义数组迭代器不执行，空洞/重复/共享/循环拒绝。
- JSON 计划传输、全量 future leaf/boundary 校验、错误 schema/身份/计数/区间/顺序/重复记录拒绝；采样输出冻结且不污染原始/运输记录。
- 400 棵种子树与另写的相对区间 oracle 对照；progress 再用逐毫秒展开重复的独立算法核对，不用生产 modulo 算法自证。
- 默认 10,000 节点宽度边界、256 层深度、字段键重排稳定性与超额 retry；大量重复不导致节点/事件数量膨胀。

[2 组真实浏览器测试](../../scripts/browser-bundles.test.mjs)比较 Node、Chromium ESM/global 和 JSON 运输结果，覆盖非 ASCII ID、zero/hold/大 repeat、未知语义和 getter/corrupt schedule 错误，禁网运行。[安装后 Node](../../tests/consumers/node.mjs)及[声明消费者](../../tests/consumers/types.ts)验证 root/browser 函数与错误、只读集合/记录、判别联合和未知 effect/target 拒绝。

交付前对抗式审查及处理：

1. **浮点溢出和巨大 repeat**：使用精确整数算术决定编译 start/end 和采样 iteration/remainder；不展开 repeat；一节点与 MAX_SAFE_INTEGER repeat 的边界/终态仍确定，溢出不会被预算/clamp 掩盖。
2. **每次 seek 隐性排序/状态依赖**：边界只在编译排序；采样用线性单调性与每叶双 bit 验证完整边界，O(leaves)，无共享 registry/playhead。JSON 跨 Node/浏览器采样一致，乱序寻址不重放事件。
3. **恶意数据/假传输计划**：描述符检查拒绝 getter、未知/不可枚举字段、稀疏/自定义数组；节点/深度/ID 字符有限。采样验证所有叶子和全部边界，包括当前时刻之后的数据。普通数据契约不宣称 Proxy 沙箱或源拓扑认证；保留 source tree 才能语义编辑。
4. **零时长/fill/重复 off-by-one**：active 严格 `[start,end)`；zero 在 start 已 ended；内部边界新 iteration 为 0 progress，final hold 为 1/remove 为 null。前/时/后和独立展开 oracle 有测试，避免除零和假终帧。
5. **范围与依赖外推**：span 不定义 effect，ID 不绑定 drawing，边界不 dispatch event；Layout 保持几何职责、格式层保留源 timing ID/XML。主 Backlog 的公开动画/原生映射前置闸门不变，数值 utility 不代替 target/effect contract 或实际播放。

验证：`pnpm build`、`pnpm typecheck`、`pnpm lint`、完整 `pnpm test` 均退出 0；Core **83**（含新增 9）、Layout 126、Parser 575、Exporter 49、SVG 19、CLI 11、dev-app 16、visual 27、browser bundles **31**（含新增 2）、timing research 10、diagram research 43、installed consumers 5、import integrations 75 等全通过。定向 Core 9/9、浏览器 2/2；文档链接/typed examples 与 `git diff --check` 通过。运行日志为本地 `.tmp/finite-timeline-*.log`，不作为原生播放证据。

下一步：ADV-06.1.2.1 事件日志/消费/页面重入契约，随后稳定目标身份与 authoring/IR/copy/delete 传输。分数/无限 repeat、autoReverse、group repeat、end/restart、easing、独立触发轨和具名 native effects 仍分项准入；不关闭整个时间线、动画或目标。


## Detached foreground event replay — 2026-10-26

推进 ADV-06.1.2.1，交付 [ADR0036](../architecture/decisions/0036-event-timeline-replay.md)、[公开 API/预算/身份/重入契约及示例](../api/event-timeline.md)。Core root/browser 新增 `compileEventTimeline`、`replayEventTimeline` 与 `EventTimelineError`，不新增文档/IR/Layout/格式动画字段、clock/listener 或 native effects。主任务的原生/authoring 依赖保持不变，纯算术功能不关闭动画父项。

语义与实现：

- 前台 stage 的 enter/with/after/页点击/对象点击显式建模，target 为同页 whole-object tuple；with 使用前驱 activation，after/readiness 使用直接前驱完整有限 end，不误用最长并发 sibling。
- Event identity 是 `(pageId,visitId,eventId)`；按完整日志非递减时戳与同刻原数组顺序求值，不自动排序。提前点击不排队，同一行只消费一次。输出冻结的已启动阶段/有限 samples、pending ID，以及每行 consumed/ignored/future 状态与原因。
- Request 必传当前 elementIds 与 visitId；所有 target/trigger source（含未来阶段）删除时拒绝。无关新增/删除允许；旧 visit 不能启动新 visit。清单只验证名义 ID 存在，删除再复用同字符串的代际身份仍须调用方新 ID 或重编译/重置，不伪称已完成结构引用维护。
- Seek/replay 对相同显式输入纯重算，不 dispatch；pause 的 active-time/log 快照保持则输出相同。新增当前时刻点击会改变输入；真实暂停点击策略/时钟/控制器留给 ADV-08，不能由纯函数隐式定义。
- 复用 finite 编译/验证/采样的私有 accounting views，不导入研究原型或重复 modulo/边界代码。全阶段 timing nodes 和标识符联合预算，完整未来计划/日志验证，单调 cursor 扫描 gate，不按每 gate 重扫日志、不展开 repeat、不保留全局状态。

[12 组 Core 测试](../../packages/core/test/event-timeline.test.mjs)：手算 stage activation 与 leaf delay、直接前驱并发反例；精确 scope/source/early/equal-time 状态表；seek/pause/new-visit 快照与冻结；当前对象删除/未来 target/trigger 失效；源输入快照与 stage-local timing ID；unknown/非法/未来日志和 getter/sparse/custom arrays；含 containers 的 aggregate 12,002-node 反例、各 quota/retry、SAFE_INTEGER 与 horizon end 失败；JSON 运输及未来计划损坏；300 个生成 program/log 与另写的事件优先状态机核对；嵌套串并行/zero/repeat/fill 全 stage span；1,000 个同刻 zero gates 与点击逐项一对一消费。复合 ID 的分隔符碰撞反例另验，不能用拼接键误判重复。

[2 组真实浏览器测试](../../scripts/browser-bundles.test.mjs)验证 Node/ESM/global/JSON/visit/current-inventory 一致、冻结及禁网、删除/重复/未知/访问器/坏 future plan 的错误码。[安装后 runtime](../../tests/consumers/node.mjs)与[声明消费者](../../tests/consumers/types.ts)覆盖 root/browser、新类型/判别联合、强制当前清单和只读结果。旧 finite utility 的 9 组用例完整回归，私有视图重构没有扩大其公开权限。

对抗式审查处理：

1. **点击重用/同刻排序**：每 gate 消耗一个已验证 log 行，cursor 单调；1,000 zero stages/同刻 distinct IDs 完整一对一，少一行则最后 gate pending。重复 tuple 拒绝，跨 scope 同 ID/含分隔符 ID 按精确 tuple 区分。
2. **旧页面和已删对象泄漏**：显式 visit 隔离、每次 current inventory，未来 target/source 同样检查；库存存在性不外推为 ID 代际或作者模型 copy/delete 正确性。
3. **坏 future 数据被过滤掩盖**：先捕获/验证完整 plan 和 log，wrong-page/visit 和未到时刻也不豁免字段/时戳/唯一性；全阶段 schedule 及 node totals 验后才有任何 replay 输出。
4. **预算、精度与失败污染**：共享 containers/leaf 节点和 ID occurrence 预算；动态 activation+span 先精确算再限额，安全整数和较紧 horizon 都有失败后重试。无外部提交、可变 playhead 或部分结果泄漏。
5. **纯评估误称播放器/native**：明确 consumed 是观察，不是 dispatch；pause 保持完整输入才同值，不声称暂停 UI、click-to-complete、独立触发轨或 native playback。Core 保持无格式层依赖，authoring/IR/Layout/格式传输仍另验。

验证：`pnpm build`、`pnpm typecheck`、`pnpm lint`、完整 `pnpm test` 均退出 0；Core **95**（新增 12）、Layout 126、Parser 575、Exporter 49、SVG 19、CLI 11、dev-app 16、visual 27、browser bundles **33**（新增 2）、installed consumers 5、timing research 10、diagram research 43、import integrations 75 等全通过。最后补入复合 ID/horizon 断言后定向重跑事件 12 + finite 9 = **21/21** 通过；无包源码追加变更。文档链接/typed examples 与 `git diff --check` 通过。过程日志为本地 `.tmp/event-timeline-*.log`，不作为原生证据。

接续 ADV-06.1.2.2：authoring/IR/Layout/格式适配和 copy/delete 的稳定目标传输；.2.3 对照源 timing 字段与实际原生播放。独立触发轨、native click-to-complete、retrigger/restart、subtargets、媒体事件和 ADV-08 控制器仍待完成。本轮不能关闭完整事件/时间线/动画目标。


## Slide-bound event identity transport — 2026-10-26

推进 ADV-06.1.2.2 的 owned whole-object authoring/IR/Layout 切片，交付 [ADR0037](../architecture/decisions/0037-slide-event-target-transport.md)、[完整契约与示例](../api/slide-event-timeline.md)。Native timing import/export/playback、子目标与完整源结构编辑未准入，任务与父项继续未勾选。

Core：slide 可用 `eventTimeline`/`setEventTimeline` 保存冻结 authored stages，`getEventTimelinePlan` 从实际 owned page/root/descendant IDs 派生有界计划；排除 shared layout objects。初始绑定在所有元素 materialize 后完成，失败回滚 slide/元素注册。新增 `replaceElement` 先准备保留 root ID 的候选，验证定时子目标/触发源与冲突，再提交 registry/collection；被引用对象/组删除拒绝，clear 后才能删。整页复制复用 allocator 的完整 descendant map，同步 page/target/trigger；object-only copy 不推断克隆整页 gate sequence。

IR/JSON/Layout：可选 `NormalizedSlideEventTimeline` 包装有独立 `pptkit-slide-event-timeline-v1` 版本，同时保留 **完整 authored source tree** 和 compiled EventTimelinePlan。不能把组 ID、empty groups 和拓扑压成区间后丢弃。Layout 验 wrapper/owner/current targets，重新编译 source，并对比完整计划；纯 flat plan 合法但与 source 漂移的反例明确拒绝。Layout 保留 detached source/plan，不应用动画。单页分页保留；会分裂 timed slide 的分页维持原表/页并报告，避免 continuation 克隆旧页目标。

格式边界：PPTX 默认抛 `PptxExportError` / event-timeline-export-unsupported，冻结 diagnostics 逐 stage 定位，先于 asset resolution/package/file writes。只有显式 `eventTimelineMode: "omit"` 才删除私有 normalized candidate 的整个 timing wrapper 并逐 stage 报告损失；再做 Layout/分页和静态输出。原 authoring 未改，静态包载荷与未带 timing 的对照相同。SVG 保持原 geometry，按 slide 报 event-timeline-static。Parser 不把 native source timing 伪映射为此字段；添加 authored timing 的 mixed edit-save 全阻止，clear 后文字可重试。

证据：

- [9 组 Core](../../packages/core/test/slide-event-timeline.test.mjs)：实际 root/group 身份、输入/IR detached/freeze、setter/init/插入失败清账、引用删除/清除、保根与 group 原子替换/冲突、三次整页 copy 后删源、object-only copy/reorder、layout-only target 拒绝及 foreign document diagnostics、已释放快照/名义 ID 复用所有权。
- [7 组跨包](../../scripts/slide-event-timeline.test.mjs)：Core→IR→JSON→Layout→replay，静态 SVG 对照；wrong owner/缺 source/坏 future schedule；分裂拒绝/单页保留；默认文件/asset-work 拒绝、显式 omit 包载荷和诊断；omit-before-pagination；Parser mixed block/retry；source group IDs/拓扑保留及合法 flat-plan 漂移反例。
- [真实浏览器 ESM/global](../../scripts/browser-bundles.test.mjs)验证 public methods、copy/IR/Layout retained metadata、delete guard/replace、static warning/refusal/omit，并禁网。安装 [Node](../../tests/consumers/node.mjs)/[types](../../tests/consumers/types.ts)验证声明、root/browser 输出、模式 union 和 readonly diagnostics/source。

对抗式审查的五项处理：

1. **语义丢失**：最终 IR 同时保留原始树与计划；独立构造自身合法但偏离 source 的计划，Layout 再编译对照拒绝。不是只核对 target 字符串或缺失组信息的区间。
2. **引用/事务/所有权**：copy 用 allocator map，不按标签/位置；新增/替换在 ledger commit 前验绑定，失败不占新 ID。registry 以弱 root-instance 所有权拒绝 released snapshot 的替换/重复释放，防止旧页面注销后来复用同字符串的 live ID；子树释放后的 ID 复用另有断言。外部旧计划/代际生命周期仍不伪称已全部解决。
3. **静默格式丢失**：默认拒绝先于文件/网络资产工作；explicit omit 对整个 stage/source/plan 报损失，文档不改，字节载荷对照通过。Parser whole-plan blocking 防止夹带写入，静态 SVG 不冒充播放。
4. **结构漂移**：timed split 不克隆不属于 continuation 的旧页 refs；single-page 保 ID 正常保留。object-only duplicate 不猜 stage clone 语义，跨文档和 native/source 结构维护继续开放。
5. **默认性能与资源**：普通 untimed insert/replace/remove 不额外复制/遍历 forest 或编译 timing；timed inventory 先界定 root/group 数量再入栈，长 ID/总字符限额在排序前检查。无新 package/dependency，Core 无 raw timing/XML，Layout/format 仅经公开入口依赖。

最终验证：`pnpm build`、`pnpm typecheck`、`pnpm lint`、完整 `pnpm test` 退出 0。Core **104**、Layout 126、Parser 575、Exporter 49、SVG 19、CLI 11、dev-app 16、visual 27、browser bundles **34**、installed consumers 5、timing research 10、diagram research 43、import integrations **82** 等全通过。最终补入“释放嵌套 ID 可由新 owner 注册”和缺失 source wrapper 断言后再跑本项 Core 9 + 跨包 7 = **16/16**，无源码追加变化。文档链接/typed examples 和 `git diff --check` 通过。日志在本地 `.tmp/slide-timeline-*`；均不作 native playback 证据。

兼容性：untimed 字段/输出保持原样；实验 IR v2 新可选 wrapper 自带 schema。结构实现 PresentationSlide 的消费者需实现新方法，未知 timing extension 须保留/拒绝/显式省略，不能静默丢弃。完整原生效果/格式映射、source round-trip、cross-document、subtarget、独立轨/对象级 gate copy、多页目标策略和播放器仍须按原任务验收；不关闭整体目标。


## Native timing source inventory — 2026-10-26

推进 ADV-06.2.1.3 / ADV-06.1.2.3 的源对照前置，交付 [ADR0038](../architecture/decisions/0038-native-timing-source-inventory.md)、[公开契约与 typed example](../api/native-timing-inventory.md)。Parser root/browser 新增显式 `inspectPptxTiming(bytes, options?)`，不改变默认 inspect/import 报告，也不赋予源编辑、Core 映射或播放权限。

实现：复用 ZIP/XML/OPC/MCE 上下文和真实 slide 顺序；保留原始一基元素序号、expanded sibling path、raw parent/nearest cTn ancestry、decoded attrs/direct text。所有 timing 后代平面保留，effective 仅指 XML 选中视图，inactive/extension 为 raw-only；未知 namespace/行为不推断原生执行。drawing/time 索引分开且逐 part 限定，规范十进制 uint32 仅作为保守 lookup profile，别名/非规范身份不绑定；subtarget/build/额外属性保留观察。全报告共享 work/records/text/findings 预算，路径先计额再构造，超额无部分成功；既有 XML/包限制与取消继续适用。深冻结普通结果显式 `nativePlayback/authoredMapping: not-performed`。

验证证据：

- [8 组 Parser](../../packages/pptx-parser/test/timing.test.mjs)：两方言 × 四样本；未知条件/文本/CDATAs/subtargets/builds；duplicate/alias/缺失；MCE/opaque extensions；四项精确预算/捕获/取消/retry；UTF16/alias prefixes/冻结/多 roots；三轮无关文字保存保持完整清单；两页相同 IDs 不串 part 且预算累计。
- [2 组真实浏览器](../../scripts/timing-inventory-parity.test.mjs)：禁网 Node/ESM/global 与独立 Chromium DOM 核对 raw index/name/attrs/text/ancestry/candidates，另验异步输入捕获、取消、并发失败隔离/retry。安装 runtime/types 验公开输出、只读声明及错误预算字段。
- [独立 Python runner](../../scripts/inspect-native-timing-inventory.mjs)以 ZIP/ElementTree 核对两份四页文件各 129 timing nodes，包含 raw path/属性/文字/祖先和候选；[manifest](../testing/native-timing-inventory-evidence.json)记录 hash/metrics/观测。原生成器版本/来源由既有 animation manifest 另证；runner 未启动原生应用，不作 playback 证据。

对抗式审查的五个风险及处理：

1. **inactive 内容误绑定**：raw-only 引用没有 effective candidates，inactive duplicate 不污染有效索引；XML effective label 不外推 native execution。
2. **别名/重复/跨页命中错误对象**：保留 lexical ID，drawing/time namespace 和 part 分离；别名不静默挑 owner，重复报 ambiguous，两页相同 ID 有专门反例。
3. **输出扩张和失败污染**：candidate/location/attributes 纳入共享 record/text/work 限额，path 先计额；精确临界、失败后 retry、浏览器并发与捕获证明互不污染。指标不冒充 wire JSON byte size。
4. **分类冒充语义/写入授权**：duration/repeat/condition 只保留字符串，subobjects/builds/未知不求值；三轮 text-save 只证无关保存保留，不准入 timing mutation。Core eventTimeline 仍为空。
5. **自证/原生证据混淆**：Chromium DOM 与 Python ElementTree 独立读源核对，原生重存输入有独立来源 manifest；静态包/清单成功不宣称 PowerPoint 播放。本轮检查 PowerPoint 仍报告 Mac locked，本轮未执行 GUI 删除。

下一步：逐效果 accept/defer/reject、相关编辑阻止矩阵、原生字段与 authored 契约对照和实际播放；源清单本身不关闭 .2.1.3/.1.2.3 或动画父项。

最终验证：`pnpm build`、`pnpm typecheck`、`pnpm lint` 和完整 `pnpm test` 全部退出 0。Parser **583**（新增 8）、import integrations **84**（新增 2）、Core 104、Layout 126、Exporter 49、SVG 19、CLI 11、dev-app 16、visual 27、browser bundles 34、installed consumers 5，既有 research/fixture/composition/copy 等全部通过。定向 Parser 8/8、浏览器 2/2、独立 Python runner 退出 0；文档链接、typed examples 与 `git diff --check` 通过。完整日志在本地 `.tmp/native-timing-full-build.log`、`.tmp/native-timing-typecheck.log`、`.tmp/native-timing-lint.log`、`.tmp/native-timing-full-test.log`；不作 native playback 证据。


## Stable paragraph-target source preservation — 2026-10-26

推进 ADV-07.8 / ADV-06.2.1.3 的 fixed-topology 段落索引保留，交付 [ADR0039](../architecture/decisions/0039-stable-paragraph-target-preservation.md)、[API/typed example](../api/paragraph-target-save.md)。新增显式 `textTargetSaveProfile: "paragraph-stable-v1"`，必须同时指定 content/text-run profile；默认 range guards 保持原样。允许已有绑定 run 的 scalar characters 修改，原样保留透明、合法范围内的 spTgt/txEl/pRg，不重写 timing、不创建 Core effects，不授权字符或结构重映射。

原始来源证明：逐 part 从既有绑定建 owner 清单，检查 raw p:sp 的唯一 cNvPr、唯一直接 txBody、直接 a:p 序列与绑定 paragraphIndex/sourceElementIndex 的实际祖先；空段落仍占原索引但 synthetic atom 不获得写权限。raw/inactive/extension 中的数值 ID 别名和 negative zero 都参与冲突检查；整页同 ID 不跨 part 合并，组内 child 单独证明。notes/table/group/diagram/chart targets 不获得本项权限。每条 raw reference 独立留 guard；一个 pRg 成功不能抵消同 owner 的 char/opaque 引用。

选项在字节/Node 文件读取前捕获；证明的 bindings/owners/paragraph records 跨 part 累计 maxReportFindings，既有 XML/包/导入/保存预算继续适用。取消/失败无部分 session 或计划。复用既有全候选 diff、原子 lexical writer、精确包差异与实际 Core 再投影；无新 package/dependency，Core/Layout 不暴露 raw格式身份。

证据：

- [12 组 Parser](../../packages/pptx-parser/test/paragraph-target-save.test.mjs)：两方言 × 三 text profiles × 三 Unicode 保存轮，完整 timing inventory 与每个未选中载荷不变；default/选项捕获与错误组合；首尾/单段/数值词法；18 类字符/未知/混合/越界/空/opaque targets；同 owner 多引用；inactive 参考/重复 owner/direct-body MCE；mixed 原子拒绝与 retry；notes/table；精确证明预算/取消、错误 paragraph 来源/不存在 atom；Node 文件捕获；跨页/组、累计跨 part 预算和 negative-zero collision。
- [2 组浏览器](../../scripts/paragraph-target-save-parity.test.mjs)：禁网 Node/ESM/global 三轮完整字节一致，独立 Chromium DOM 核对真实目标 owner、0..2 范围、三段顺序/文字与完整 timing；default/char guards、mixed/cancel/retry。安装 runtime/types 消费者调用公开 option/输出并拒绝未实现的 character profile。
- [独立/native runner](../../scripts/inspect-paragraph-target-save.mjs)与[manifest](../testing/paragraph-target-save-evidence.json)：Python ZIP/ElementTree 三轮精确核对所有未选中载荷及整棵 XML fingerprint（仅两 approved a:t 例外），故意修改 range/无关属性均被 oracle 拒绝。具名 LibreOfficeDev 26.8.0.0.alpha0 保留三段文字，但各轮重存把本样本 pRg 0..2 改为 0..0；**未编辑原文件对照同样改写**，因此 native range observation 明确为 partial。最后原生保存结果重开两页 PDF，changed/control text 均在；未执行 GUI/实际播放。

对抗式审查的五项风险与处理：

1. **把字符偏移误当段落索引**：只准透明单一 pRg，charRg/混合/未知/越界仍 block，Unicode/换行仅改变现有 a:t；不推断语义段落移动。全部 normalized topology/property 改动仍由原整候选分析拒绝。
2. **来源错配/隐藏身份碰撞**：真实 raw atom preorder 与直接 paragraph 祖先核对，故意错误 paragraph/missing atom 拒绝；所有 raw cNvPr 含 inactive alias/negativezero 冲突则不证明，part/组有独立反例。
3. **放开一条引用掩盖另一条**：逐条 raw target 决策，不按 owner blanket grant；额外 char/opaque、相关 notes/table 或 mixed unsupported mutation 都阻止完整计划，无关 owner 仍可 retry 保存。
4. **预算/取消/文件入口遗漏**：证明记录跨 part 累计且建段落 map 前计额，所有扫描/入栈检查取消；边界耗尽、注入取消、public abort/retry、异步选项/bytes 和实际 Node file capture 都验证。默认 profile 不增加此证明遍历。
5. **native 成功假象与变更归因**：独立 fingerprint 验证 PPTKit 的源保留，native 重存另列；对照证明 sample range 改写在无编辑时也出现，不归因于本次 scalar 保存，更不以 retained text/PDF 声称 native播放。相关父项继续未完成。

最终验证：`pnpm build`、`pnpm typecheck`、`pnpm lint`、完整 `pnpm test` 均退出 0。Parser **595**（新增 12）、import integrations **86**（新增 2）、Core 104、Layout 126、Exporter 49、SVG 19、CLI 11、dev-app 16、visual 27、browser bundles 34、installed consumers 5，既有研究、fixture/composition/copy 等全部通过。最后补充错误 raw paragraph/missing atom 与跨 part 预算断言后，定向 12/12 再次通过，无追加源码改动。独立 Python/native runner 退出 0，native range 仍 partial。文档链接/typed examples 和 `git diff --check` 通过。日志为本地 `.tmp/paragraph-target-full-build.log`、`.tmp/paragraph-target-typecheck.log`、`.tmp/paragraph-target-lint.log`、`.tmp/paragraph-target-full-test.log`、`.tmp/paragraph-target-focused-final.log`、`.tmp/paragraph-target-independent.log`。下一步接字符/段落结构目标策略和具名 timing/native 播放对照，不关闭 ADV-07.8/ADV-06 或完整目标。


## Atomic table structure transactions — 2026-10-26

推进 AUT-05.2.1/.2/.3 的当前直接样式模型，交付 [ADR0040](../architecture/decisions/0040-atomic-table-structure-edits.md)、[API/typed example](../api/table-editing.md)。Core 新增纯 `editTable(table, operations, { frame, limits? })`，返回冻结候选和完整原始 explicit anchor mapping；不直接修改 document，调用方通过既有 replaceElement 提交。完整边框/主题、cell级高级目标和源结构写回仍开放，父项不勾选。

行为：以物理 grid 坐标按顺序执行插删行列、矩形 merge、任意覆盖坐标 split。跨插入点的 merge 扩 span，边界插入只移动 anchor；删除锚点行列但 merge 尚有剩余时原内容/样式提升到剩余 top-left，完全删除则 mapping 无 target。默认 merge 拒绝 non-anchor 非空/动作内容；显式 append-paragraphs 保留 row-major rich paragraphs/runs/styles/actions 与空段落，要求相同 text preset；split 仅在原 anchor 留内容，新 unit cells 为空并独立复制 style/preset。frame 必须显式 preserve/fit-grid，原缺省行高按原 frame/原 row count 一次物化。原 implicit blanks 转为显式 unit cells，但不编造原始身份。

映射：每个原 explicit anchor 的 rowIndex/compact cellIndex/physical columnIndex 都有 retained/merged/deleted 终态；merge→split 的原合并内容来源继续指向 top-left，不伪称恢复旧格。该批地址不是长期 cell ID/OOXML provenance，不授权 source-save，也不猜 native subtarget/comment bindings。root ID、动作、transform/accessibility 等由 candidate 保留，whole-object event target 在 replace/copy 后遵循原 registry 契约。

资源与失败：descriptor capture 拒绝 getter/sparse/custom arrays/cycles/非 plain 值；各中间状态 rows/columns/slots/cells 有上限，capture values/key+text characters 及 work/depth 跨输入、operations、物化和最终结果累计；sort 在执行前计固定 n-log-n 成本，避免 JS 引擎排序策略导致相同 quota 结果不同。错误携带 path/code，原输入/live root 不变，无部分输出或隐藏定时器。

独立集成发现并修复既有 Exporter 缺陷：混合 rowspan/colspan 超过两列时，active merge entries 使用了已经递增的 column cursor，后续 vertical continuation 被放错列，导致 fresh PPTX 连 Parser 重开都失败。现在按稳定 anchorColumn+offset 放置；[独立物理槽回归](../../packages/pptx-exporter/test/table-merge-continuations.test.mjs)覆盖 spans 2/3/4/7、有/无 leading column、3-row merge 与后续完整一行。不是放宽 Parser 来接受错误包。

证据：

- [13 组 Core](../../packages/core/test/table-edit.test.mjs)：内外/边界插入、锚点提升/完全删除、最后行列拒绝、冲突/部分 merge 拒绝、rich append/空动作/不同 preset、split 内容样式隔离、implicit blanks/缺省尺寸、非法分区、getter/cycles/sparse arrays、九项 exact quota/retry、live root/whole-object timeline/copy删源。200 组双轴 inverse；3,000 次多 merge partition 的混合操作与另写的 **物理二维数组 splice oracle** 对照，检查完整 compact grid/内容/尺寸与 retained/deleted；四种操作均实际执行超过 250 次。
- [4 组跨包](../../scripts/table-edit-parity.test.mjs)：三批 fresh export/import/reopen/no-op；Node/ESM/global 完整候选/映射与精确 work quota 边界一致；禁网 Chromium DOM 独立核对每个物理 tc 的 hMerge/vMerge/span、尺寸、正文和 XHTML anchor。编辑后 measurement/pagination 保持 merge block、正文不重复，authoring 未被 Layout 修改；源结构保存仍整批拒绝，restore no-op 后合法 scalar 可 retry。安装 runtime/types 验 root exports、必选 frame、operation/limit union、readonly mapping。
- [独立/native runner](../../scripts/inspect-table-edits.mjs)与[manifest](../testing/table-edit-evidence.json)：三批候选用 Python ZIP/ElementTree 逐物理槽、全部 continuation、paragraph content 和尺寸核对，故意改坏 continuation 均被 oracle 拒绝。具名 LibreOfficeDev 26.8.0.0.alpha0 三批重存 grid/content pass；最大列宽误差约 **0.0221 pt**、行高约 **0.0268 pt**，在显式 0.1 pt 研究阈值内。最后保存结果重开两页 PDF，剩余/新增文字与 control 均在，Delete B/D 不在，Tail 恰三份。未执行原生 UI 编辑；fresh 输出不作 source structure preservation 证据。

对抗式审查的五个风险与处理：

1. **合并区边界与内容丢失**：first-free compact→physical partition 验重叠/越界；跨线扩大、删 anchor 提升、部分 merge 拒绝。二维 splice oracle 不复用 span 变换算法；显式 delete 的完全移除和合并来源完整反映在 mapping。
2. **rich text/preset/action/空样式**：default 不吞 non-anchor 内容，零文字动作也阻止；append 保留完整段落/run，preset 不同拒绝，merge 的 cell-wide style 明确 top-left；split 不复制正文到所有格。未实现完整 border/theme 规则，不关闭 AUT-05.1。
3. **失败半提交与身份污染**：全部变化在 detached private state，失败无候选；replaceElement 管 root commit/引用，失败 live root unchanged，整页 copy 后删源仍保留独立表格。cellMapping 不冒充稳定 cell IDs/native reference permission。
4. **资源/引擎差异**：所有中间 grid states 限额，复制模板/append/最终报告都计入累计 data，sort 固定 charge；九项 exact boundary、两浏览器形式精确 work quota 与失败后 retry。无文件/网络/clock 副作用。
5. **格式自证与 gate 外推**：独立 DOM/Python 发现真实 continuation bug 并从 writer 修复，native 重存按0.1 pt独立列误差；source-save block/restore/retry 单独测试。PDF/重开不是原生交互编辑证据，结构保存仍由 IMP-09.3.4 独立完成。

最终验证：最终源码的 `pnpm build`、`pnpm typecheck`、`pnpm lint` 和完整 `pnpm test` 全部退出 0。Core **117**（新增13）、Exporter **50**（新增1）、独立表格跨包 **4**、Parser595、Layout126、SVG19、CLI11、dev-app16、visual27、browser bundles34、installed consumers5、import integrations86，以及既有 research/fixture/composition/copy 等全部通过。最后修正 rich paragraph fixture 为真实 `align` 字段并增加 Core 实际归一化的 align/bold/italic/preset/action 断言后，定向 **13/13** 再通过，无追加运行时逻辑变化。精确 work quota 的 Node/ESM/global 边界在最终跨包检查通过。独立 Python/native runner 三批通过，最后 PDF remaining/deleted/Tail数量断言也通过。文档链接/typed examples、`git diff --check` 通过。最终日志为本地 `.tmp/table-edit-final-build.log`、`.tmp/table-edit-final-typecheck.log`、`.tmp/table-edit-final-lint.log`、`.tmp/table-edit-final-test.log`、`.tmp/table-edit-focused-final.log`、`.tmp/table-edit-independent-final.log`。

接续：完整 table border/theme precedence、稳定 cell identity/高级引用、具名原生 UI 编辑，再推进 Parser IMP-09.3.4 的源结构保留 writer。当前 fresh 原生表格输出与纯 Core 事务不能关闭这些任务或完整目标。

## Direct table borders and shared physical grids — 2026-10-26

推进 AUT-05.1.1 / IMP-06.1.4 的直接样式切片，交付 [ADR0041](../architecture/decisions/0041-shared-table-borders.md)、[API/typed example](../api/table-borders.md)。Core 新增稀疏四边 overrides，缺省属性继承 uniform stroke；无 borders 的旧表保持旧路径，空对象显式 opt-in。Layout 统一解析 physical edges 和精确 cell geometry：可见、显式、宽度、dash rank、upper/left 依次优先，同一 merge 内部隐藏。slot/edge/work 限额跨请求内所有 opt-in 表累计，所有分配先计额；prefix geometry 避免旧 cell-overlap 的平方遍历。frame/grid 不一致给 located warning，不偷偷拉伸。

SVG 用精确 HTML cells 与共享 SVG lines，fill alpha/root opacity/transform 各应用一次。Parser 两方言把支持的 unequal/incomplete direct borders 投影为 sparse edges，完整 uniform merged perimeter 可聚合；异色/部分缺失 segments 明确 source-only，旧无 continuation styles 的 anchor-only projection 兼容保留。Scalar text source saves 原样保留边框 XML，style/structure 写回仍阻止。

独立原生实验发现并修复：单纯 physical slot encoding 让 wholly merged anchor 的 lnR/lnB 成为 noFill，原生丢失右/底边。现在 anchor 写 uniform logical outer sides，continuations 仍写 physical hints；不均匀边 anchor noFill 并发出 located `table-border-segmented-native`，不假装完整 native parity。Office 要求的 horizontal continuation rowSpan / vertical continuation gridSpan 同步输出。另因 Office 忽略 graphicFrame rotation/flips，fresh transformed table 改为原生 group + local editable table，root ID/action/accessibility 保留一次，private child ID 不冲突，opacity 不平方，`table-transform-grouped` 报告层级变化。三轮导入和浏览器像素回归已适配该明确层级，不移除内容/像素断言。

证据：

- Core 4 组：稀疏归一化/继承、旧 IR 兼容、非法边/paint/dash/arrows；修复非法 null paint/color 导致 TypeError 而非 validation diagnostic。
- Layout 7 组：冲突次序、merged interiors/分段边界、detached 几何、exact 三类 quota 和跨表累计、错误 partition/style、空格/nested/local geometry、frame mismatch。
- Parser 4 组：两方言、多轮 scalar saves、完整 physical merge outer sides、异色 segments source-only/原 XML 保留、旧 anchor-only 与 source-style reject/retry。
- [跨包 4 组](../../scripts/table-borders-parity.test.mjs)：三轮 fresh RGB graph；Node/ESM/global exact work quota；独立 Chromium DOM 检查每个 physical/logical anchor edge、Office spans、SVG segments/rgba/单次 opacity；editTable/sizing/pagination；JSON Layout corruption rejects；source-style mutation 整批阻止。
- Exporter 回归覆盖 whole merge spans 2/3/7 四边和隐藏 interior，以及 transform group/native IDs/action/alpha；已有 spans2/3/4/7 continuation 检查增加 Office span 断言。安装 runtime/type consumers 覆盖公开 API/limits/readonly/unknown fields。
- [独立 runner](../../scripts/inspect-table-borders.mjs)与 [manifest](../testing/table-borders-evidence.json)：Python ZIP/ElementTree 核对完整源物理边、逻辑锚点和 merge metadata，故意改宽度必拒绝；具名 LibreOfficeDev 26.8.0.0.alpha0 重存、原输入直接 PDF 对照和 PyMuPDF/PNG 核查。三 RGB 场景几何颜色 coverage 12/12、20/20、12/12；whole merge 右/底边缺失已修复。严格 width/alpha checks 仍 partial：PDF widths 改变、忽略 stroke opacity，重存 alpha/noFill widths 改写、whole merge3x3 折为1x1。transform scene source/control 和 resaved PDFs 均有 geometry/flip 差异且 PNG 确认；不校准 canonical widths/alpha 来迎合具名 producer。

对抗式审查的五项风险与处理：

1. **直接规则冒充 Office cascade**：优先序、noFill 不压邻格 visible、upper/left tie 均有反例；table style/banding/theme 仍未准入，不关闭 AUT-05.1。
2. **merged perimeter 与 physical interiors 混淆**：独立 native 实际揭示丢边，再修 writer；anchor logical side 与 continuation physical hints 分别检查，不用 same-owner 邻格相等掩盖锚点语义。不均匀 sides 显式 warning/source-only，不能以单个样本宣称全支持。
3. **重复 opacity/transform 与身份冲突**：group root/child 分离、IDs 唯一、单次 action、逐 line alpha 独立断言；浏览器原投影像素一致，三轮 native wrapper 不增长。LibreOffice transform discrepancy 仍记录 partial，不等于 native 编辑成功。
4. **网格资源/metadata 错误**：allocation 前共享计额、线性 ownership/prefix geometry、精确 quota/retry；frame mismatch 仅报告，JSON unknown/truncated/missing geometry 拒绝，不让两端悄悄用不同边框。
5. **fresh/source/native 证据互相代替**：三轮 fresh graph、scalar source XML 保留、style write blocked、native XML/PDF/control 分开；源未知内容、完整主题、原生 UI 与 source writer 闸门保持开放。此批未执行 GUI 删除或编辑。

接续：完整 AUT-05.1.1 cascade → AUT-05.1.2 theme/direction/source provenance → IMP-06.1.5 具名 native interaction/segmented/transform 证据 → IMP-09.2.5 style/metric source writer，再接 IMP-09.3.4。完整目标和父项继续开放。

最终验证：本批最终源码的 `pnpm build`、`pnpm typecheck`、`pnpm lint`、完整 `pnpm test` 均退出 0。Core **121**（新增4）、Layout **133**（新增7）、Parser **599**（新增4）、Exporter **52**（新增2）、SVG19、CLI11、dev-app16、visual27、browser bundles34、installed consumers5、table-edit跨包4、table-borders跨包4、import integrations86，其余 research/fixture/composition/copy 等通过。补充横向 upper-owner tie 与分段 warning 位置信息断言后分别定向7/7与三轮 fresh 用例通过，无运行时逻辑追加。独立 Python/native runner 退出0但 native strict fidelity 保持 partial；最终文档/typed examples 和 `git diff --check` 通过。日志在本地 `.tmp/table-borders-final-build.log`、`.tmp/table-borders-final-typecheck.log`、`.tmp/table-borders-final-lint.log`、`.tmp/table-borders-final-test.log`、`.tmp/table-borders-transform-regressions.log`、`.tmp/table-borders-native-final.log`。中途失败为旧测试假定 table 永远顶层/无 warning，均改为验证明确 wrapper 与实际状态后重跑完整套件通过；未降低逻辑内容/浏览器像素/身份检查。当前全面目标仍 active，本批结果不表示所有 TODO 已完成。

## Format-independent table region style materialization — 2026-10-26

推进 AUT-05.1.1 的创作区域求值，交付 [ADR0042](../architecture/decisions/0042-table-region-style-materialization.md)、[API/typed example](../api/table-style.md)。Core 表格新增 table-local `style`：wholeTable、物理 bandedColumns/bandedRows、首尾行列、四角、outer/inner border roles 和 text paragraph/run defaults。归一化一次求值，Layout/SVG/PPTX 共用 effective cells，无新包、依赖、Office GUID 或 mutable registry。

优先级：whole → table-border roles → column bands → row bands → first/last columns → first/last rows → topLeft/topRight/bottomLeft/bottomRight → cell text preset → cell/style/content。paint/判别联合原子覆盖，stroke/margins 逐属性，后层 uniform stroke 先 patch 所有 inherited side candidates 再应用逐边；undefined 继承，零/false/noFill/alpha0 不丢失。物理 anchor 选择 band，整块 merge 的触边判定首尾/角；单行单列同序求值。presence `{}` 也启用 header/footer 排除，body band 起点明确。

有界 compact proof 在分配前要求 1..100000 physical slots，验证矩形、覆盖与越界，线性物化 implicit blanks。空内容数组/zero-run 段落也建立空 styled run；不复制 merge 正文，不创造 source cell IDs，作者快照不变。无style保留旧路径。`editTable` 保留 root rules 后按新拓扑重算；测量看到 effective fonts/margins，分页重复 header/保留 source body stripe，不能每页重启 band 或把每页末行都变 lastRow。

IR marker `styleSource: "materialized-regions"` 仅标 effective values 来源，不保留 unresolved rule object 或伪称逐属性 provenance。fresh PPTX 写 effective direct cells/theme refs，located `table-style-materialized` 明示原生 reusable rules 不保留；重导入不能恢复原作者抽象。主题角色跨两个 document 继续 semantic，直接局部RGB不变。源导入 session 添加规则仍整批阻止保存，restore baseline 后 no-op 可重试。完整 source/native cascade 与 source writer 未准入。

证据：

- [8组 Core](../../packages/core/test/table-style.test.mjs)：完整区域次序、局部字段组合/zero/false/alpha、2D merge 物理外边、band size/exclusions、退化 corners、implicit/explicit blanks、文字预设/富文本/空段、undefined/detach/规则删除/编辑重算、closed grammar 与 exact100000 slot边界。**160**独立矩形分区以几何 membership oracle 检查所有 anchor 最终fill，不复用 compact traversal。
- [4组跨包](../../scripts/table-style-parity.test.mjs)：三轮RGB fresh effective fill/text/margin/span/edge graph + exact source no-op；Node/ESM/global canonical 完全一致与禁网；独立 Chromium DOM 检查实际 native fill/run flags/font size 与 SVG每个 cell paint/text；两个主题document semantic role/direct override；edit→measurement/pagination及source拒绝/restore。测量夹具同时保证 fixed rows 可容纳内容，避免触发正确的 preserve-on-overflow 后误判autoRows未生效。
- 安装 runtime/types 消费者实调用 regions/materialized warnings，未知native ID/region字段 TS拒绝。
- [独立 runner](../../scripts/inspect-table-style.mjs)与[manifest](../testing/table-style-evidence.json)：RGB17、theme17、inserted21 anchors。Python ZIP/ElementTree逐源anchor核对fill/text/run bold/italic/font size/color/margin，故意改fill必须拒绝。具名 LibreOfficeDev26.8alpha headless 三场景 saved fill/text pass，保存PDF中指定header/merged/body/control文字均在；RGB/theme PNG实际查看。**只证明fill/text**，不提升为border/alpha/transform/native-edit pass；截图仍可见producer分段边行为，上一批native partial继续有效。本轮没有GUI删除/交互编辑。

对抗式审查的五项风险与处理：

1. **把自定义规则伪装Office继承/原生复用**：Core不接GUID，IR只effective cells+origin marker，fresh有located materialization warning，重导入不伪造规则；源/native继承父项继续开放。
2. **merge/blank/band边界错位**：physical anchor + rectangle-touch 分开；implicit blanks及显式零run空内容参与；whole merge/单行列/双轴与160几何oracle覆盖，内部线继续交Layout隐藏。
3. **局部假值/paint/stroke优先序漂移**：undefined过滤、paint atomic、margin逐边、uniform再side逐层patch；false/zero/alpha0、局部preset+内容有断言。相邻格共享边仍用既有Layout规则，不能把一格local override理解成强制邻格候选失败。
4. **编辑/分页把设计冻结到错误位置**：root规则保留并按新网格重算；测量实读local12pt+resolved2pt margins；分页保留source stripe/header，作者快照不变。源样式修改阻止+restore no-op重试单独验证。
5. **资源/格式自证与native范围外推**：allocation前slot cap、闭合规则语法和located errors；Node/browser/installed与Python/DOM独立读取输出，故意mutation校验oracle。native只fill/text，未验证line widths/alpha/segment/transform/interactive或source style writer，不因三个pass关闭父项。

下一步：IMP-06.1.4 具名源table-style/theme/flags的effective cascade与来源追踪；AUT-05.1.2 样式身份/复用、方向、主题局部覆盖；IMP-06.1.5真实原生改样式/插行/换主题保存；IMP-09.2.5 direct styles/metrics source writer及完整未知引用证明。全面目标与相关父项继续开放。

最终验证：最终运行时源码的完整 `pnpm test` 退出0：Core **129**（新增8）、Layout133、Parser599、Exporter52、SVG19、CLI11、dev-app16、visual27、browser bundles34、installed consumers5、table-edit跨包4、table-borders跨包4、table-style跨包4、import integrations86及既有research/fixture/composition/copy全部通过。最后补充null local style诊断断言后，Core8+cross4共 **12/12** 再通过，无运行时修改。最终构建/typecheck/lint、文档链接/typed examples与`git diff --check`均通过；独立Python/native runner在最终构建复跑退出0，三场景fill/text/PDF text保持pass，native reusable rules仍unsupported、交互untested、source-style writes blocked。日志为本地 `.tmp/table-style-final-build.log`、`.tmp/table-style-final-typecheck.log`、`.tmp/table-style-final-lint.log`、`.tmp/table-style-final-test.log`、`.tmp/table-style-focused-final.log`、`.tmp/table-style-native-final.log`、`.tmp/table-style-final-diff.log`。本轮推进创作区域求值，不宣称所有TODO或全面目标完成。

## Source table style/theme direct-region projection — 2026-10-26

推进 IMP-06.1.4 的源直接区域样式，交付 [ADR0043](../architecture/decisions/0043-source-table-style-projection.md)、[API/typed example](../api/table-style-import.md)。Parser 新增 `tableStyleInheritance`：inline/reference/default definition选择、规范GUID/六flags、逐cell fill/border/text的选中来源与依赖。验证内部whole-part tableStyles关系、实际 `presentationml.tableStyles+xml` 类型、root/MCE；不猜 built-in定义，重复effective身份拒绝，explicit坏ID不退default。结构projection不执行此求值。

源cascade独立于Core自定义创作规则：whole→horizontal band→vertical band→first/last columns→first/last rows→corners→local cell/list/paragraph/run。border按源region及merge rectangle的outer/inside求值，物理continuations沿用完整grid证明。未知更高paint/font/line/style-matrix明确阻断旧字段；不把旧颜色伪装继承成功。conditional仍preview fallback，merge有anchor/boundary approximation；heterogeneous直接perimeter显式阻断继承logical side而非露出旧线。

复用目标slide/layout/master的theme override/color-map/Latin字体/ordered color transforms；外部style definition提供owner path map，避免把tableStyles当slide根读取。region值缓存按目标theme scope分开；额外把external reader的consumption/diagnostic状态也按scope隔离，防止一套缺色palette给另一套有效palette留下错误audit。三个session-wide limits覆盖选择+属性、依赖、index/path/audit/decl/cell traversal，现有ZIP/XML/theme/MCE/report/save预算继续独立生效。

保存：原style GUID/flags/XML/theme/relationships不进入Core authoring rules。no-op原字节不变，显式V3允许既有bound run的scalar字符修改；源style/metric/structure mutation仍整批block，restore后retry。fresh只带effective direct格式，不能从中还原作者源style。有效路径不冒充raw MCE XPath或writer权限；代表性uniform physical边来源也不冒充完整continuation清单。

证据：

- [10组Parser](../../packages/pptx-parser/test/table-style-inheritance.test.mjs)：两方言×inline/reference/default、flags/全部regions/local override、theme palette/map/override与Latinfont/false/alpha；whole/region inner-outer borders；unsupported高层阻断、missing/duplicateGUID/external/wrongtype/badbool/MCE；三轮scalar source bytes；三类exact session预算/失败retry；两个master共享定义的颜色与audit隔离（含一个palette unsupported的负例）；bytes/options异步捕获和取消；conditional merged heterogeneous side 阻断+保存原styles。
- [2组跨包](../../scripts/table-style-import-parity.test.mjs)：Node/ESM/global两方言三轮完整source保存与完整projection/provenance一致、禁网；独立Chromium DOM定位每个selected源fill并核对真实值。每轮未修改payload精确保留；fresh effective格式与原生style definition丢失分开，source mutation block/restore单独验。安装runtime/types消费者实调用report/limits/readonly声明。
- [独立runner](../../scripts/inspect-table-style-import.mjs)与[manifest](../testing/table-style-import-evidence.json)：三个由完整generated package改成真实source table-style references的控制，Python ZIP/ElementTree独立核对source selected fill路径/颜色，故意修改选中颜色或使其路径失效必拒绝；具名LibreOfficeDev26.8alpha重存direct fills、原输入对照PDF与重存PDF文字/PNG。初次native发现dual-band内部四格horizontal-over-vertical错误，改成source vertical later后，regions/theme/local各 **16/16 fills pass**；两个PDF均保留16个控制文字，region/theme PNG实际查看。只证明fill+文字存在，不证明文字颜色/typography、border/alpha/transform/native复用/PowerPoint或交互。默认文字appearance仍开放。

对抗式审查的五项风险与处理：

1. **把Core创作cascade直接套源格式**：独立native找出四格错误并修正source order；仍标conditional fallback，不用具名LO覆盖所有PowerPoint/merge/overlap情况。style-matrix/default appearance另列，不关闭父项。
2. **身份/关系/MCE错误选择**：有效GUID唯一、inline/reference冲突拒绝、缺/坏显式不偷用default，真实content type校验；MCE未知分支不进effective索引。source profile保留路径/definition而不造Core native IDs。
3. **跨master颜色及diagnostic污染**：theme-scope缓存+external reader隔离；双master同styleID不同RGB、一个不支持palette与另一个仍有效的audit反例通过，dependency指到实际theme/map/override。
4. **不支持字段退旧值/merge丢边/写权限外推**：高优先级不支持声明阻断，heterogeneous physical边阻断 inherited logical边；完整raw绑定保存仍只scalar字符，style mutation原子拒绝，三轮未改样式载荷精确保留。
5. **预算/取消或自证假象**：选择/selected props+deps跨part累计、work在checkpoint检查、exact上下界与bytes/options捕获/abort/retry；DOM/Python不调用Parser求值实现，mutation guard曾发现验证器改在另一个解析树上未生效，已改为实际改动被核对的source树且缺路径显式拒绝。native direct-fill pass不扩大为text appearance/interactive/source-style-write pass。

下一步：theme fmtScheme/fillRef/lnRef和placeholder色、源默认text appearance与script字体、更多native overlap/merge/RTL矩阵与PowerPoint实操，再在未知引用保留证明后开放IMP-09.2.5 style/metric source writer。相关父项及完整目标仍开放。

最终验证：最终源码 `pnpm build`、`pnpm typecheck`、`pnpm lint` 和完整 `pnpm test` 全部退出0。Parser **609**（新增10）、Core129、Layout133、Exporter52、SVG19、CLI11、dev-app16、visual27、browser bundles34、installed consumers5、table-edit4、table-borders4、table-style4、import integrations **88**（新增2），其余research/fixture/composition/copy均通过。最终定向10+2共 **12/12**，scope负例、merged阻断及inline身份断言均通过。独立Python/native runner在最终构建复跑退出0，三个source控制各16格fill pass，source-control与saved PDF文字存在性通过；相关完整native/style-write闸门仍开放。中途完整流程一次遇到Cycle旧截图用例的Chromium captureScreenshot协议错误，几何断言已通过；原用例未改，单独2/2和随后完整流程全部通过，没有删除截图检查或把该次错误冒充pass。最终docs/typed examples、边界检查与`git diff --check`通过。

日志为本地 `.tmp/table-inheritance-full-build.log`、`.tmp/table-inheritance-typecheck.log`、`.tmp/table-inheritance-lint.log`、`.tmp/table-inheritance-full-test.log`、`.tmp/table-inheritance-focused-final.log`、`.tmp/table-inheritance-native-final.log`、`.tmp/table-inheritance-cycle-retry.log`、`.tmp/table-inheritance-diff.log`。本轮没有GUI交互编辑、提交或发布；全面目标继续active，不能以此关闭所有TODO。

## Source table theme-matrix references — 2026-10-26

推进 IMP-06.1.4 的 format-matrix 切片：[ADR0044](../architecture/decisions/0044-table-theme-matrix-references.md)、[API/typed example](../api/table-style-import.md#theme-format-matrix-extension)、[五控制独立证据](../testing/theme-matrix.md)。Parser 私有 paint/line 模块解析 fillRef/lnRef，theme resolver 独立选择 format/color/font 组件并 lazy 索引原始有效位置。无新增包/依赖/Core native 身份或 public report schema；保留既有 profile 和 source-style 保存守卫。

fill0/1000→noFill，fill1..999→普通list、1001起→backgroundlist；line0→noFill/width0、其余只选line list。未知slot不跳位，近层空fmtScheme不退旧matrix。phClr只按需解析引用色，固定矩阵色忽略未用引用；palette/reference/matrix modifier保持连续program，最后一次RGB量化，alpha set/mod/off保序。reference idx是selected source，真实theme entry及色/变换为dependencies；unsupported theme audit立即进入全局finding预算并保留真实part/path。

证据：

- [10组Parser](../../packages/pptx-parser/test/theme-matrix.test.mjs)：两方言/index边界与sentinel、独立component/override、未知位置/MCE、phClr缺失及fixed不用引用、连续色/alpha、线语义/真实theme诊断路径、两master隔离、三轮source scalar保存/style拒绝、exact累计预算与取消retry。黑色+tint99500+shade50000明确区分正确080808与错误中间量化090909，避免弱oracle只测两种算法恰巧相等的输入。
- [2组跨包](../../scripts/theme-matrix-parity.test.mjs)：Node/ESM/global双方言完整值与provenance、三轮未改payload精确保留、独立DOM实际idx/list slot/phClr/line width检查、禁网、budget/options捕获/abort/retry。安装runtime消费者实查matrix值与lnStyleLst来源；文档typed example包含idx-source筛选。
- [Python/native runner](../../scripts/inspect-theme-matrix.mjs)：五场景每个80 selected properties独立核对source及故意index mutation拒绝。具名LO placeholder/no-line两份PDF各16标签和fill sample pass；XML线宽仍partial。background/transform source-control和resaved都保留实际偏差；fill1000重存和source PDF应用exit1，记unavailable。无GUI操作，不把错误归为审批拒绝；三个PNG已实际查看，未调整canonical期望去迎合producer。

对抗式审查五项风险与处理：

1. **索引误移/大索引分配/错误退旧值**：按实际effective children保留未知slot，先实际长度检查；unsigned极值、普通/背景/line分界、近层缺list和高层unsupported均有反例，不选替代slot。
2. **placeholder重复量化/alpha顺序丢失**：私有source program连接原RGB与modifier序列，一次最终量化；独立公式和真实080808/090909反例，alpha重设/乘/偏移分别断言；program不泄漏report或Core。
3. **引用/主题来源错位或审计失真**：selected source=ref idx，依赖指实际theme；neutral属性明确消费，未知cmpd等仍在theme路径即时审计，避免全记在reference或延后绕过finding cap。双master缺失/有效matrix隔离测试通过。
4. **source保存权限意外扩大**：三轮只允许既有bound scalar text，style/theme/关系payload精确不变；改样式阻止整批，restore后retry。fresh effective输出不冒充原生registry/source writer。
5. **独立检查通过被外推native兼容**：Python source pass与native status分列；五控制逐stage记录，背景/alpha/width偏差和noFill应用失败保留。PDF内点不证明边线/字体，未做PowerPoint/插行/换主题交互；父项不勾选。

最终完整 `pnpm build`、`pnpm typecheck`、`pnpm lint`、`pnpm test` 全部退出0：Parser **619**（新增10）、Core129、Layout133、Exporter52、SVG19、CLI11、dev-app16、visual27、browser bundles34、installed consumers5、table-edit4/table-borders4/table-style4、import integrations **90**（新增2）；research/fixture/composition/copy既有组也通过。定向12/12通过。最后文档/typed example更新另跑 docs check 与 diff check；日志 `.tmp/theme-matrix-final-build.log`、`.tmp/theme-matrix-final-typecheck.log`、`.tmp/theme-matrix-final-lint.log`、`.tmp/theme-matrix-final-test.log`、`.tmp/theme-matrix-focused-final.log`、`.tmp/theme-matrix-native.log`。

接续[四张执行卡](advanced-capability-todos.md#表格主题矩阵之后的具体执行卡)：默认文字/脚本字体、更多矩阵paint/line、PowerPoint/区域语义与既有格直接style/metric source writer；仍继承完整前置，未实现的SmartArt、动画与全面目标继续active。没有提交或发布。

## Table reference text appearance and explicit uniform fonts — 2026-10-26

推进 IMP-06.1.4.1 与 IMP-05.1.3.3.2 的显式字体切片，交付 [ADR0045](../architecture/decisions/0045-table-text-appearance-and-uniform-fonts.md)、[API/typed example](../api/table-style-import.md#reference-text-color-and-explicit-script-fonts) 和[独立source/PDF观察](../testing/table-text-appearance.md)。Parser 原先漏掉 fontRef 默认色，并在显式 table font collection 中只看Latin；现在引用色→region直接色→后续region/local按字段覆盖，RGB/alpha一起替换；字体idx与颜色资格独立。不造省略属性的native默认值。

theme resolver新增显式major/minor lt/ea/cs slot解析，保留target scope/独立font component及checkpoint；空/缺slot不猜脚本fallback或退旧component。共享run字体读取器仅在Latin/ea/cs解析为相同family时折叠，table collection再核对supplemental entries；不同/缺失/空/不支持的显式alternative阻断旧family，同值dependency可追踪，重复script拒绝。没有增加Core多字体模型或将字体替代写回source。纯fontRef仍保持原Latin投影边界，不宣称自动选择主题supplemental字体。

证据：

- [12组Parser](../../packages/pptx-parser/test/table-text-appearance.test.mjs)：双方言的fontRef源色/alpha/idx来源、region/local覆盖与alpha0/opaque reset、高层unsupported阻断/独立idx颜色、直接/主题同字体集合、多字体/空/缺slot阻断且后续相等不复活、supplemental同值/差异/重复、独立component/override、local shape混合文本、双master隔离、三轮scalar保存与style阻止/restore、exact work/dependency预算和capture/取消/retry。Core/Layout/源保存模型未改。
- [2组跨包](../../scripts/table-text-appearance-parity.test.mjs)：Node/ESM/global双方言引用色/显式集合、三轮source bytes/provenance一致、禁网；Chromium DOM独立读取源路径/主题字体；三轮fresh XML核对每个真实run颜色和Latin family。安装包消费者使用public exports读取显式ea来源。
- [独立runner](../../scripts/inspect-table-text-appearance.mjs)与[Python oracle](../../scripts/research/oracles/table-text-appearance.py)：四控制各8 selected properties，真正改动被检查树中的选中颜色必须拒绝。具名LO所有conversion进程成功，explicit/local/collection的两份PDF各四文字/颜色通过；reference-only源PDF却为白色、重存为黑色，均不等于预期accent2。实际字体LiberationSans/约15.987pt，不冒充Arial/16pt外观pass。reference source PNG实际查看几乎空白，explicit saved PNG有四标签；独立提取文字存在不能掩盖白字不可见。
- 全量回归先暴露三项旧比较器把字体theme引用与具体family当作不相等；现按明确fontFamily字段解析有效family，其余字段保持比较，新增一组错误family必失败、独立source theme/Latin/ea/cs XML和三轮no-op/fresh验证。定向 `import-content-parity` **13/13**，没有删除字体断言或去掉style比较。

对抗式审查五项风险与处理：

1. **为一个颜色丢失alpha或越过局部覆盖**：reuse已有paint evaluator，reference/default先、region/local后；unsupported阻断两个字段、显式opaque/alpha0分别断言。idx不支持仍可独立映射颜色。
2. **把多字体集合冒充Latin/自动脚本选择**：全部显式alternatives核对；不同/空/缺/未解析阻断、重复拒绝、同值保留deps；补local shape与两master反例。Core仍one-family，自动script/language/fallback/shaping任务未关闭。
3. **更近font component缺slot后回退/跨master污染**：原component-unit规则不变，显式ea/cs只选当前component；partial override不混旧slots，双master一缺一有效分别验。
4. **测试为新行为让路而掩盖错误字体**：fresh只比较有效family，另留原IR theme引用断言、错误family负例、独立XML；source no-op/三轮未改payload仍精确字节验证，不以语义相同替代保留保证。
5. **native程序成功或文本可提取被算外观pass**：四控制逐scene/源/重存分列；reference白字与重存黑字、字体替代明确partial；三项颜色pass不扩大为alpha/字体/shaping/PowerPoint或原生交互pass。source style写回仍原子blocked，scalar修改后可恢复重试。

接续默认省略值/空段与脚本字段的格式无关传输、language/script选择、实际shaping/缺字形和PowerPoint default-color/改样式/换主题对照；IMP-06.1.4.1及其父项继续开放。全面目标active，无GUI编辑、提交或发布。

最终验证：最终运行时源码 `pnpm build`、`pnpm typecheck` 和 `pnpm lint` 退出0；补齐有效字体比较与明确夹具accessibility后，完整 `pnpm test` 退出0：Parser **631**（新增12）、Core129、Layout133、Exporter52、SVG19、CLI11、dev-app16、visual27、browser bundles34、installed consumers5、table-edit4/table-borders4/table-style4、import integrations **93**（新增2 appearance +1 effective-font），既有research/fixture/composition/copy均通过。source/typed docs检查与 `git diff --check` 通过。中途三处表示比较失败及新fixture缺显式accessibility说明的断言均如实保留为修复过程，不作为pass证据；最后13/13定向和完整93/93已覆盖修复后的比较器。

日志为本地 `.tmp/table-text-final-build.log`、`.tmp/table-text-final-typecheck.log`、`.tmp/table-text-final-lint.log`、`.tmp/table-text-final-test.log`、`.tmp/table-text-focused.log`、`.tmp/table-text-font-parity.log`、`.tmp/table-text-parity.log`、`.tmp/table-text-native.log`、`.tmp/table-text-source-oracle-final.log`、`.tmp/table-text-docs-final.log`。本轮没有把局部实现或三项原生颜色通过等同于所有TODO/全面目标完成。

## Explicit script-font authoring and shared selection — 2026-10-26

推进 IMP-05.1.3.3.2 / IMP-06.1.4.1 的格式无关传输与创作层选择，交付 [ADR0046](../architecture/decisions/0046-script-font-authoring-and-selection.md)、[API/typed example](../api/script-fonts.md) 和[独立native观察](../testing/script-fonts.md)。Core新增两个可选family字段，与原fontFamily分开继承；undefined继承、authoring null重置单slot，IR省略空slot，不改变旧IR形状。各owner/preset/placeholder/table region验证、克隆、归一化、复制与JSON传输均接线，无新增包/依赖/源格式身份。

共享纯 `resolveTextFontSegments` 基于明确命名的Unicode脚本集合、组合符/连接符及neutral上下文选择实际family；保留UTF16偏移、原文本与空插入段，跨surrogate不截断。maxSegments在追加前检查，线性codepoint遍历、仅按segment建输出array；未知/未列脚本仍default，runtime Unicode版本及language/Han变体/supplemental规则不冒充完整Office策略。

Layout仅将选中的具体fragment交font coverage resolver与冻结measurement request，保留原run/style/身份；缺某script字体或selection失败不提交部分body测量，fallback诊断指原script字段。SVG两条文字路径使用同一选择器，HTML一个author action只包一层，CSS家族字符串对quote/backslash/control编码。fresh PPTX不按preview分段，而是在原run写Latin/ea/cs三个typeface；Core heading/body role仍指已建模Latin family，故保持+mj-lt/+mn-lt而不偷换成不同主题字体。

证据：
