- [8组Core](../../packages/core/test/script-fonts.test.mjs)：不可变snapshot/detach/duplicate/notes/JSON，preset/placeholder/shape/table region独立继承与null重置，direct/preset/shape/cell/notes全部非法值定位、closed table grammar；无slot旧IR兼容；36个具名Eastern/complex/other字符控制、supplementary Han、combining/ZWJ/emoji/neutral/newline、UTF16连续拼接/theme role/空串、exact预算和100000同脚本文字。
- [3组Layout](../../packages/layout/test/script-fonts.test.mjs)：实际三字体fragment/provider冻结、原run未改、缺字体整body阻止与fallback located path、交替脚本超额不发送部分measurement request/不改几何。
- [4组跨包](../../scripts/script-fonts-parity.test.mjs)：Node/ESM/global canonical/layout/原生bytes/SVG/segment完全相同及禁网；三次fresh独立Chromium DOM核对text/shape/table五run+notes一run所有真实字体槽、theme/reset，SVG native和XHTML字体片段、action仅一层；三轮source no-op精确原bytes，现Parser unequal-source-family仍unsupported，新source style修改blocked/restore；CSS引号/控制符不能创建额外color声明。已接入完整test:script-fonts脚本。安装runtime/types使用公开字段/helper、readonly segments与非法theme-role编译负例。
- [Python/native runner](../../scripts/inspect-script-fonts.mjs)：对照独立author字段而非调用selector/writer，ZIP/ElementTree核对6 source runs，故意改ea family必须拒绝。具名LO进程均成功，但slide run **5→15**、部分font槽省略；两份PDF用LinuxLibertineG/DejaVuSans替代synthetic字体。提取Latin/中文/Arabic均在，但两份PNG实际查看CJK不可见；native/PDF统一标observed，source检查独立true。未用文本存在性或打开成功冒充字体/字形/身份pass。

对抗式审查五项风险与处理：

1. **新字段破坏旧IR/继承或重置全局字体**：optional字段不提供就省略，flat字段继承现有独立cascade；null只清一个slot；preset/placeholder/region/local正反例和默认单family路径单独断言。Core heading/body仍原语义，native不换成未建模的script theme集合。
2. **脚本猜测、组合符切开或无界分配**：公开精确支持字符集合与neutral规则、不称Office全覆盖；named字符/supplementary/组合/ZWJ/空串/边界测试，UTF16完整重建；追加前maxSegments、同script长串不建per-character array。runtime Unicode和language/substitution差异明确留闸门。
3. **measure/preview修改作者run或丢失action**：fragment仅是派生值，provider收到具体family和实际coverage文本；缺font不测量，实际字段path定位。原run数/normalized snapshot保持，native仍原run；HTML action只按sourceRun包一层，浏览器独立DOM断言。
4. **font name进入CSS/XML造成声明污染**：新slot名字非空/无C0与DEL，theme对象闭合；XHTML CSS同时编码quote/backslash/control，XML沿用escapeXml。浏览器解析恶意family后颜色仍123456；没有为通过测试删除输入/属性检查。
5. **Core能传输被外推成source语义或native可编辑/字形正确**：Parser现阻断保持，三轮source bytes与style guard单独验；native5→15与CJK不可见如实留observed。缺字体、shaping/bidi、language/script family source profile、PowerPoint实操与source字体writer均未被本轮关闭。

接续独立source字体profile的逐字段cascade/provenance/unsupported阻断、两方言/多master/三轮source scalar保存；之后language/Han/supplemental选择与实际已安装字体coverage/shaping/原生UI准入。所有完整父任务与全面目标继续active，本轮无GUI编辑、提交或发布。

最终验证：最终源码完整 `pnpm build`、`pnpm typecheck`、`pnpm lint`、`pnpm test` 全部退出0。Core **137**（新增8）、Layout **136**（新增3）、Parser631、Exporter52、SVG19、CLI11、dev-app16、visual27、browser bundles34、installed consumers5、script-font跨包 **4**、import integrations93，以及既有table/research/fixture/composition/copy均通过。最后补齐全部命名脚本字符控制后定向 **15/15**，包含placeholder/null reset和36 named codepoint controls。最后文档/typed example与`git diff --check`通过。

日志为本地 `.tmp/script-font-final-build.log`、`.tmp/script-font-final-typecheck.log`、`.tmp/script-font-final-lint.log`、`.tmp/script-font-final-test.log`、`.tmp/script-font-focused-final.log`、`.tmp/script-font-docs.log`、`.tmp/script-font-native.log`。原生manifest的source slot true与native/PDF observed保持分开，没有把缺字或font substitution改写成通过；完整TODO仍未完成。

## Explicit source script-font profile — 2026-10-26

推进 IMP-05.1.3.3.2 / IMP-06.1.4.1 的源侧字段切片，交付 [ADR0047](../architecture/decisions/0047-source-script-font-profile.md)、[API/typed example](../api/script-font-import.md)、[独立source/native观察](../testing/script-font-import.md)。新增显式 `textFontProfile: "script-families-v1"`，byte/file入口在首个await前验证/捕获，content报告带资格marker；不提供时保留原single-family行为与报告形状。没有增加Core字段、包、依赖或XML身份；复用上轮Core脚本字体与本包source/provenance预算。

run/default-run Latin/ea/cs按字段独立cascade，支持直接值和target theme component显式槽。local Latin不会清掉继承的ea/cs；更近空/坏/未解析slot阻断自身，不显露旧script值。selected theme scheme缺slot可追踪实际component/node依赖。table显式collection作为choice替换，缺required槽阻断；fontRef独立选三slot。无法表达的collection supplemental规则阻断，theme supplemental另逐源节点发source-only finding，不静默做language选择。近层font component不混旧slot。

新增profile按目标theme scope隔离继承reader的consumption/notes，避免共享master/list的一次unsupported解析污染另一个有效slide。direct notes可记录同一全局text-property/dependency预算下的来源；notes theme引用仍unsupported，不借slide主题。候选验证携带捕获的profile重新投影实际bytes，仍比对完整Core snapshot，不删除font字段来得到save pass。权限仍只来自既有text-run profile；font/style新增/删除/修改与文字混改全部原子阻止，恢复可重试。

证据：

- [14组Parser](../../packages/pptx-parser/test/script-font-import.test.mjs)：双方言直接独立slot/default兼容、master/list/paragraph/run字段覆盖、空段endParaRPr插入style、unsupported高层不退旧值、不同theme slots和近层partial缺依赖、table collection/reference/local、partial/supplemental边界、duplicate/MCE、direct notes与禁止借slide主题、三轮混合owner scalar保存、三类font写入阻止/restore、两master及同source跨slide scope审计、精确累计text/notes/table property/dependency/work预算、Nodefile/bytes选项捕获与取消retry。theme supplemental控制保留namedslot projection同时有真实theme-part/path的source-only finding。
- [3组跨包](../../scripts/script-font-import-parity.test.mjs)：Node/ESM/global双方言三轮source plan/完整bytes/provenance一致及禁网；Chromium原始DOM XPath独立读取实际字体reference、theme slot和dependency值；三轮fresh混合text/shape/table/notes有效fonts与真实native slot核对；browser mode错误/budget/捕获/取消/default retry。首次DOM验证因未绑定presentation前缀而失败，补实际源命名空间解析后通过，未删source XPath或property断言。
- 安装runtime/types消费者通过公开profile/marker/新property union，实际prepare/save一次scalar text，未知profile编译负例；Node file adapter保留调用时captured profile。
- [runner](../../scripts/inspect-script-font-import.mjs)和[Python oracle](../../scripts/research/oracles/script-font-import.py)：原始+三保存共四包，各 **18字体property / 6文字leaf** 源值与approved状态一致；只有slide1/notesSlide1变动，fixture中所有a:t字符内容之外bytes和其余part payload/part集合均精确不变。每包故意只改一个ea typeface，两套独立font-value和non-character-byte检查都拒绝。此mask仅证明具名generated fixture，不冒充任意XML writer。
- 具名LO所有conversion退出0，最终五slide owner标签和可提取Chinese/Arabic均在，但native run **5→15**，实际fonts为LinuxLibertineG/DejaVuSans。source-control与resaved PNG实际查看仍CJK不可见；notes owner6由source XML检验、不期待slide PDF显示。native/PDF保留observed，未改成typography/coverage/pass。

对抗式审查五项风险与处理：

1. **破坏旧profile或把projection当权限**：显式content-only新flag，bytes/file同步capture，marker仅opt-in；default旧值/旧blocker继续测，editSave未授权仍blocked。没有替换默认导入或放开font writer。
2. **用一个font字段覆盖整个集合/缺slot露出旧值**：三key独立patch；Latin-only不清ea/cs，unsupported仅阻断自身，collection choice缺字段不混旧集合；partial theme node/schema依赖留证，endParaRPr只style空插入run。
3. **共享source audit污染/notes错借theme/忽略supplemental**：新profile继承reader per-theme scope，两个master和同master跨slide override负例；notes只direct，不造notes母版继承；collection supplemental保守阻断，theme额外规则source-only并受finding/work预算，不推断language。
4. **保存复验丢profile后误拒绝或削弱验证**：profile进入session edit state、Nodefile config和actual candidate projection；三轮mixed owner保存通过完整snapshot，不删除字体/主题断言。style mutation全量blocked、restore/retry；独立XML/lexical byte mask反向注入证明源字体不能被顺带改写。
5. **绿色source/原生进程隐藏字体缺字与拓扑改变**：source/property结果与native observed分列，five labels可提取不等于Chinese可见；synthetic字体替代、run拆分、notes不在slide PDF均记实。真实字体coverage/shaping/bidi、language/Han/supplemental/notes继承及PowerPoint UI验收继续开放。

接续language/supplemental/Han变体的格式无关契约与逐源选择、具名已安装字体coverage/缺字形/shaping、完整notes master/theme和PowerPoint改样式/换主题；source字体writer仍需原独立任务准入。所有完整父任务及全面目标继续active。本轮没有GUI交互、提交或发布。

最终验证：最终运行时源码完整 `pnpm build`、`pnpm typecheck`、`pnpm lint`、`pnpm test` 全部退出0。Parser **645**（新增14）、Core137、Layout136、Exporter52、SVG19、CLI11、dev-app16、visual27、browser bundles34、installed consumers5、script-font authoring跨包4、import integrations **96**（新增3），其余既有research/fixture/composition/copy/table组均通过。最后补充空段插入style断言后，14+3定向 **17/17** 再通过，无运行时修改。独立runner退出0，四包source/font/字符边界校验通过，native/PDF仍observed。最后文档/typed examples和`git diff --check`通过。

日志为本地 `.tmp/script-font-import-final-build.log`、`.tmp/script-font-import-final-typecheck.log`、`.tmp/script-font-import-final-lint.log`、`.tmp/script-font-import-final-test.log`、`.tmp/script-font-import-profile-final.log`、`.tmp/script-font-import-focused-final.log`、`.tmp/script-font-import-native.log`。本轮仅推进显式源slot契约，不宣称所有TODO或完整脚本字体/原生目标完成。

## Script font maps and explicit language selection — 2026-10-26

推进 IMP-05.1.3.3.2 / IMP-06.1.4.1 / AUT-01.3 的格式无关选择切片，交付 [ADR0048](../architecture/decisions/0048-script-font-maps-and-language-selection.md)、[API](../api/script-fonts.md#script-font-map-extension) 和 [typed example](../examples/script-font-map.ts)。先解决三slot不能在一个作者run表达Arabic/Hebrew不同family的问题；不把source supplemental仅根据当前文字物化，避免后续scalar文字改动隐式变成字体style改动。

Core新增37准入script key的可选map、frozen公开key集、逐key/全map reset及有界共享选择。只读取显式Intl.Locale组件，不maximize或查询宿主locale/font。Han按显式script、ja/ko及zh/yue地区选择；bare zh/invalid/不兼容script保守Hani。Hira/Kana→Jpan、Hang→Kore、Traditional Bopo→Hant；marks/neutral/UTF16及CR/LF/LS/PS边界显式定义。旧slot-only/单font路径保持兼容。table regional merge保留reset barrier。没有新增包或依赖。

Layout仅测量阶段物化fragments，provider不接收map规则，缺失/替代定位原作者map key，失败不部分测量。SVG双路径共享选择并保留一个run action wrapper。fresh PPTX将当前字符串具体family写到三native slots、邻接同family合并，保留style/lang/alpha/action。每text owner/notes/layout placeholder发`script-font-map-materialized`，即使当前字符串未使用map；native reusable map/theme linkage和原run拓扑不保留。默认Parser与script-families-v1未扩大source语义或字体写权限。

验证证据：

- 新增8组Core：snapshot/copy/notes/shape/JSON、preset/placeholder、table区域reset与后续map不复活旧key、7类owner定位错误、全部37key具名字符/alias、简繁/日/韩与ambiguous/invalid/private-extension/显式script优先级、组合/joiner/punctuation/UTF16/CR-LF-LS-PS/精确预算和旧path。
- 新增1组Layout：Arabic/Hebrew/Hant独立fragment、missing/fallback完整诊断、provider无map与作者快照不变。
- 新增4组跨包：Node/ESM/global完整canonical/Layout/bytes/warnings/SVG一致且禁网；独立browser DOM读取实际source XML三slot/alpha/每fragment action及SVG/XHTML；三轮fresh generation current fonts保持、map/topology明确loss、source exact no-op与map编辑blocked/restore；空插入与当前unused map警告、default旧theme link不变。新夹具首次把action type误写成hyperlink，原生链接断言拒绝后改为公开url类型；没有移除action断言。
- isolated installed runtime/types消费者验证公开map/code、主题值、unknown-key/normalized-null编译负例。typed example通过文档编译。

对抗式审查：

1. **全map清空后后续一个key复活旧值**：regional merge保留全key reset屏障，合法wholeTable→firstRow null→corner object与preset/local key删除均有回归。局部cell.style.text属于非法API，额外测试首次被校验拒绝，改用合法region链验证而未放宽闭合语法。
2. **locale猜测改变文字或宿主结果**：不maximize，显式脚本/地区优先级与ambiguous/invalid/扩展控制均测；格式内source规则尚未解读。Unicode新字符归类仍依赖runtime版本。
3. **line/paragraph separator沿用上一字体**：审查发现map模式LS/PS会被neutral规则带入上一字体，改为和CR/LF一致default/Zyyy并重置；保留旧slot-only路径，四类边界回归。
4. **物化丢规则而虚报round-trip，或片段丢action/alpha**：显式warning覆盖每owner与unused/empty字串；独立DOM查16slide+4notes字体片段及alpha/actions。三轮只比较当前具体font，不声称还原map/原run身份；source map写入仍blocked。
5. **source成功冒充真实字体/native成功**：本轮无native GUI/新原生排版验收；旧synthetic缺字/substitution仍observed。source supplemental/notes/真实coverage/shaping/PowerPoint theme-edit叶任务全部开放。

本轮把后续工作拆成IMP-05.1.3.3.2.1–.6，并写入[TODO](advanced-capability-todos.md#脚本字体映射的接续任务)、next-delivery与ROADMAP。只有.1限定authoring/selection/materialization切片完成，完整父任务与全面目标保持active。此前Cycle空白节点原生删除/保存/重开已有独立证据，本次不重复GUI删除；没有提交或发布。

最终验证：LS/PS边界修正后的最终源码完整 `pnpm build`、`pnpm typecheck`、`pnpm lint`、`pnpm test` 全部退出0。Core **145**（新增8）、Layout **137**（新增1）、Parser645、Exporter52、SVG19、CLI11、dev-app16、visual27、browser bundles34、installed consumers5、script-font跨包 **8**（新增4）、import integrations96及其他既有research/fixture/composition/copy/table组均通过。随后仅修改文档说明，typed examples/链接检查与 `git diff --check` 通过。本轮没有新的native字体外观结论。

日志为本地 `.tmp/font-script-map-final-build.log`、`.tmp/font-script-map-final-typecheck.log`、`.tmp/font-script-map-final-lint.log`、`.tmp/font-script-map-final-test.log`、`.tmp/font-script-map-final-docs.log`；新夹具修正后的定向与reset复验日志为 `.tmp/font-script-map-focused.log` / `.tmp/font-script-map-reset.log`。不宣称所有TODO完成。

## Source supplemental-font map projection — 2026-10-26

推进 IMP-05.1.3.3.2.2/.3 / IMP-06.1.4.1，交付 [ADR0049](../architecture/decisions/0049-source-supplemental-font-projection.md)、[API](../api/supplemental-font-import.md)、[typed example](../examples/supplemental-font-import.ts) 和 [独立source证据](../testing/supplemental-font-import.md)。没有把当前字串的脚本选择烘焙为source样式；新增显式`script-families-v2`，default/v1保留既有语义与报告边界。

Parser把原始collection supplemental元信息留在private native-slot声明中，先完成Latin/EA/CS字段cascade，再按最终声明投影各script key。显式局部literal/unsupported槽替换该bucket的旧map，缺省EA/CS可以由最终Latin collection提供其规则；matching theme tokens携带collection，cross-slot tokens按具名具体槽投影。空theme ea/cs仍是unsupported generic字段，同时有效具体脚本条目可独立mapped；这不等于猜Latin。近层fontScheme整组件替换、table collection/fontRef/conditional/local choice、major/minor分别选择。Core只有已准入37key的具体family字符串；source/provenance不进入Core。

`run.scriptFontFamilies.<code>`报告实际supplemental typeface、script身份、selector/generic-slot/collection依赖与原target；expanded properties/dependencies计入原text/notes/table累计预算。private Map不会泄漏进报告。未知script和未消费属性/子节点source-only；坏已知typeface仅阻断对应key；无script身份阻断整个admitted map；重复选择identity含unknown重复拒绝。MCE effective分支先选，theme/component与shared style缓存scope隔离。无新package、dependency、字体安装或宿主查询。

session与Nodefile沿用同步capture，把v2带进实际candidate重投影，完整Core snapshot比较而不mask字体字段。font/map/lang/style新增、变更、删除混入scalar编辑仍整体blocked；restore可retry。fresh输出独立物化并警告rule/theme-link/topology loss，不重建source collection。notes只保持direct边界，不借slide theme。

验证：

- [11组Parser](../../packages/pptx-parser/test/supplemental-font-import.test.mjs)：双方言/default/v1兼容、完整map与当前text无关、空generic槽与支持key分开、继承和局部literal/坏槽/major-minor/cross-slot、未知/空/控制字/recursive typeface及重复、近层组件/双master/同source跨scope、direct/fontRef/conditional表格、缺required slots但有具名key、own dictionary替换referenced dictionary、effective MCE与未知charset审计、三轮scalar与map/lang/style原子阻止、direct notes、byte/file option capture/cancel、精确text/table property/dependency/work边界和retry。
- [3组跨包](../../scripts/supplemental-font-import-parity.test.mjs)：Node/ESM/global双方言三轮完整canonical/provenance/bytes一致且禁网；独立DOM XPath检查14项实际map值/script/dependency/target并故意改坏Hant再拒绝；mixed text/shape/table/notes三轮source exact payload/字符外byte、fresh native三slot与SVG和物化warning；browser capture/limit/abort/invalid/no-writer/restore。
- installed tarball runtime/types消费者通过公开v2/options/map-property union，unknown script property编译负例和一次真实scalar prepare/save。没有为测试增加Exporter到Parser的运行时依赖；首次测试误从未安装的包名导入，改用既有跨包测试模式。
- [runner](../../scripts/inspect-supplemental-font-import.mjs)与纯stdlib [Python oracle](../../scripts/research/oracles/supplemental-font-import.py)：原始+三次source保存各 **29字体规则 / 6 scalar文字leaf**，实际script/typeface/selector/dependency/target存在且值一致；part集合不变，只有slide1/notesSlide1字符内容改变，其他byte和part payload精确保留。故意将Hant改为Wrong Source，同时被map源值和exact byte两套检查拒绝。具名fixture mask不冒充通用XML writer。manifest和hash在[记录](../testing/supplemental-font-import-evidence.json)，本地artifact为`.tmp/supplemental-font-import/run-7bQSXk`。

对抗式审查五项：

1. **过早按文字选字体破坏scalar保存或局部override留下旧map**：private关联跟随native字段cascade到finalization，不查询text/lang；局部EA/CS/literal/unsupported、缺省Latin fallback及collection整choice分别测试，三种不同脚本字串保存仍same map/full snapshot。
2. **空槽误报成功、未知key被吞或坏identity借旧值**：空generic仍unsupported；已知key逐项、未知source-only、missing script整map阻断、duplicate拒绝；direct table缺generic仍保留有效key并报告三个generic blocker，不放宽旧v1。
3. **跨scope元数据/诊断污染或private Map流进公共报告**：multi-master/override/bad-scope独立测试，所有报告无fontMap私字段；notes无theme借用。source supplemental优先级是声明的experimental profile，尚需native对照，不借文档结构说明证明应用行为。
4. **独立检查自证或fresh输出拓扑断言过强**：XPath/Python直接读取实际XML并注入错Hant；初始fresh测试把selector片段误当native run一一对应，遇到既有同family coalescing而拒绝，修正具名fixture的zh-TW语言并按真实family合同独立断言，未改变writer来迎合测试。source保留与fresh警告分开。
5. **只有绿测便宣称原生字体/全部任务完成**：本轮没有native/GUI字体检查，manifest明确native和coverage/shaping未测试；.2/.3 source自动化已有实证，但具名native优先级、notes theme/master、真实字体shaping及PowerPoint改样式/换主题仍开放。字体writer不授权，所有完整父任务与全面目标继续active。

最终运行时源码完整`pnpm build`、`pnpm typecheck`、`pnpm lint`、`pnpm test`全部退出0。Parser **656**（新增11），Core145、Layout137、Exporter52、SVG19、CLI11、dev-app16、visual27、browser bundles34、installed consumers5、script-font authoring跨包8、import integrations **99**（新增3）及既有research/fixture/composition/copy/table组均通过。最后补充control/partial/referenced-dictionary测试后11组定向再通过，没有runtime修改。随后仅文档/记录更新，typed examples/链接与`git diff --check`复验。

日志：`.tmp/supplemental-font-full-build.log`、`.tmp/supplemental-font-typecheck.log`、`.tmp/supplemental-font-final-lint.log`、`.tmp/supplemental-font-final-test.log`、`.tmp/supplemental-font-focused-final.log`、`.tmp/supplemental-font-independent.log`。本轮无GUI删除、提交或发布。下一步先做native font precedence具名对照和notes独立字体继承；字体coverage/shaping与源字体writer保留各自准入条件。

## Independent notes-master text inheritance — 2026-10-26

推进 IMP-05.1.3.3.2.4 / IMP-06 notes字体与文本继承切片，交付 [ADR0050](../architecture/decisions/0050-notes-master-text-inheritance.md)、[API/typed example](../api/notes-style-import.md)、[独立source证据](../testing/notes-style-import.md)。新增content-only `notesTextProfile: "master-style-v1"`，而不是静默扩大default/字体v1/v2的direct notes语义。没有Core模型、package或dependency新增。

先核对Microsoft的PowerPoint占位符规则：notes→notesMaster按 **type**，与slide→layout的idx匹配不同。因此用独立bounded notes-master scan，校验解释到的drawing identities/placeholder indices，仅跟随recognized drawing containers并缓存candidate/count；不从extension islands制造候选。唯一非group的body类型可以提供placeholder list，ambiguous/missing/grouped只记录边界并保留独立global notesStyle。source idx1/master idx99具名控制仍正确匹配；parent prompt paragraphs不复制成Core notes。已有local直接body唯一性/缺text边界不放宽。

继承链为实际notesMaster notesStyle defaults/level → type匹配body lstStyle → local notes lstStyle → paragraph → run/end insertion。notes master和local source levels/dependencies均可追踪，placeholder字段额外保留实际两端type attr依赖。字体按选择的default/v1/v2 profile解释；notes/master主题component、notes clrMapOvr与notes-master clrMap独立解析，不借slide/layout/master主题，也不拿presentation列表中的master替代缺失的实际关系。近层缺slot/坏component阻断，shared master不同target scope保持reader/audit隔离，即使未选择显式font profile。

Node/byte/file在首次await前捕获新flag，实际save candidate重新投影保留notes与font两profile，以完整Core snapshot比较。既有V2/V3 scalar notes permission仍独立，font/map/lang/style混改整体blocked，restore/no-op可重试。fresh output只写effective local notes样式，script map警告物化，不复原原始master/theme/完整notes页面。

审查发现XML source大小不能独自限制同master在不同notes override下反复求值，新增累计 `maxNotesStyleWork`（默认1000000），覆盖opt-in matching/inherited list/audit与notes theme/font/color求值；缓存不重复计相同工作，direct-only模式不消费此profile预算。所有text/notes仍共享原property/dependency quota，source XML/MCE/report/snapshot/save/cancel预算继续生效。

验证证据：

- [13组Parser](../../packages/pptx-parser/test/notes-style-import.test.mjs)：双方言/旧flag不变、独立notes字体/色与来源、按type而非idx、local list/paragraph/run独立覆盖/unsupported、notes list层级与empty/end insertion、missing/ambiguous/grouped parent、缺master/theme不借slide/列表、非法/重复drawing/placeholder ids、extension islands、effective MCE与未消费属性、共享与不同实际master/theme、notes color-map override、近层font component和无font profile时audit隔离、三轮scalar/混style阻止、无writer permission、byte/file capture/cancel、text property/dependency和notes work精确累计边界与retry。
- [3组跨包](../../scripts/notes-style-import-parity.test.mjs)：Node/ESM/global双方言三轮full canonical/report/bytes一致且禁网；独立XPath读取6 map/6 generic fonts/2 placeholder fontsize实际源与dep/target、actual notes颜色映射及idx1/99；错font控制拒绝。mixed两notes+一drawing scalar保存精确payload/字符外byte，fresh native notes当前四字体片段/22pt/RGB与map-rule loss分开检查；browser invalid/capture/limit/abort/restore/default retry。
- packed runtime/types消费者通过公开notes选项、新source levels和work quota，未知profile编译负例，Node文件导入后一次实际scalar prepare/save。首轮测试抓到file adapter漏传notes flag，已补capture/config转发；误把raw author缺省font当normalized Aptos的测试已按真实API分层修正；OPC fixture误拼object被control XML校验拒绝后改用relationship builder；no-op测试使用相同Uint8Array视图比较真实bytes而不把Buffer原型差异当字节丢失。
- [runner](../../scripts/inspect-notes-style-import.mjs)与纯stdlib [Python oracle](../../scripts/research/oracles/notes-style-import.py)：原始+三scalar保存四阶段各 **22 selected style properties / 2 notes texts**，实际OPC notesMaster/theme关系、type匹配、字体/语言/字体大小/斜体/色与alpha源值、source/dependency/target均检查。只有两notes和slide1字符内容改变，master/theme/rel与其余payload/part集合以及允许part字符外byte精确保留。每阶段故意改坏notes Hant字体或master body type，两控制均被实际style检查和exact byte检查同时拒绝。named fixture mask不是通用XML writer。最终manifest在[证据](../testing/notes-style-import-evidence.json)，本地`.tmp/notes-style-import/run-nbDaj6`。

对抗式审查五项：

1. **照搬slide idx匹配或复制master prompt**：用独立type scanner及1/99控制，ambiguous/grouped/extension/missing正反例，Core无MASTER PROMPT。source prompt/frame仍明确未映射，不虚报整页notes。
2. **notes借slide主题或跨scope污染**：只走实际notes关系，两个不同master/theme及缺关系/near component/本地color-map覆盖；无font profile时的unsupported color audit也隔离，所有新levels source路径和notes依赖独立检查。
3. **新flag在文件入口/候选复验丢失**：实际file测试先暴露遗漏再修正；同步capture、bytes/file调用后篡改option和三轮save完整snapshot回归，没有mask继承字体/颜色或降低candidate比对。
4. **共享master重复求值资源放大**：增加专用累计work budget，缓存+checkpoint与精确阈值/一少拒绝/retry验证，direct-only最小quota仍通过，不把table预算挪给notes。
5. **绿source测试冒充native appearance或style writer**：独立OPC/XML和双负例只证明named source，fresh native XML字体片段不等于应用字形/布局；manifest nativeAppearanceAndEditing仍untested、fullNotesPageLayout unsupported、notesStyleWrites blocked。所有完整父任务和全面目标继续active，原生字体/shaping/PowerPoint改样式换主题另行验收。

最终work-budget运行时源码完整 `pnpm build`、`pnpm typecheck`、`pnpm lint`、`pnpm test` 全部退出0。Parser **669**（新增13），Core145、Layout137、Exporter52、SVG19、CLI11、dev-app16、visual27、browser bundles34、installed consumers5、script-font authoring跨包8、import integrations **102**（新增3），其余既有research/fixture/composition/copy/table组均通过。13+3 notes定向及独立oracle通过；随后仅文档/记录变化，typed examples/链接与 `git diff --check` 通过。无GUI编辑、提交或发布，完整目标继续active。

日志：`.tmp/notes-style-final-build.log`、`.tmp/notes-style-final-typecheck.log`、`.tmp/notes-style-final-lint.log`、`.tmp/notes-style-final-test.log`、`.tmp/notes-style-budget-final.log`、`.tmp/notes-style-independent.log`、`.tmp/notes-style-final-docs.log`。下一步继续真实字体coverage/shaping与具名native font/notes优先级、PowerPoint编辑换主题对照；完整notes页面和source style writer保留各自准入闸门。

## Installed-font shaping and native selection controls — 2026-10-26

推进 IMP-05.1.3.3.2.5/.6，新增[实测说明](../testing/real-fonts.md)、[20控制builder](../../scripts/research/font-native-cases.mjs)、[LO runner](../../scripts/inspect-real-font-native.mjs)/[独立oracle](../../scripts/research/oracles/real-font-native.py)、[真实字体runner](../../scripts/inspect-real-font-shaping.mjs)与[具名deck/原生轮次oracle](../../scripts/research/oracles/font-native-deck.py)。没有生产代码/API/依赖或默认profile修改，不开放source font/style writer。

**真实字体：** 固定实际已安装font的file/index/PS/version/hash和cmap ranges；TTC face用HB keep-everything本地提取，源face与提取face的完整glyph/cluster/offset/advance必须一致。相同字节由FontFace加载，禁网，CDP实际custom PS和glyphCount验证可见DOM而非只看CSS声明。8组简繁日Han、韩文Hangul、Arabic带mark/lam-alef、Hebrew带mark和Latin combining全部actual face匹配、无glyph zero、Layout无diagnostic；48px下HB/browser宽度最大差0.01225px，Layout/DOM差小于0.02px。Arial真cmap拒绝Han与emoji，未提供bold拒绝，允许具名Han fallback产生准确slide/element/path诊断；author runs不变。最终[manifest](../testing/real-font-shaping-evidence.json)与本地`.tmp/real-font-shaping/run-iAgNLm`保留元数据/PNG；截图已查看。仅regular与孤立RTL样本，mixed bidi、emoji完整shaping和SVG不冒充完成。

**native环境：** 最初六slide默认bundled LO出现CJK空白且PDF用LinuxLibertine/DejaVu；给独立child-process FONTCONFIG_FILE指向实际host fonts后CJK可见。配置/cache均留在单run，不安装字体、不修改全局设置、不把旧缺字结果追认为pass。LO26.8 alpha的20控制source/resave/reopen PDF进程退出0、源slots/hash独立检查通过，但多项theme supplemental选Arial Unicode MS/Arial，与v2不同；详见[LO manifest](../testing/real-font-native-evidence.json)。不据此更改Parser来迎合单应用。

**PowerPoint：** 用户解锁后处理临时文件本地访问窗口。继承的AppleEvent导出请求已超时且无PDF，确认终态后改用CUA原生UI导出。单页explicit-sc实际PS为HiraginoSansGB-W3。首份合并deck触发修复提示，取消而未接受修复；修正研究夹具为常规part位置和全局唯一master/layout数值ID。原20个slide/theme payload hash不变、所有internal关系闭合，再次打开成功，无修复提示。PP实际版本从installed plist读取为16.104/build16.104.25121423。

20页native PDF实际font结果为**15匹配/5差异**：[manifest](../testing/powerpoint-font-selection-evidence.json)保留ko-Han→MicrosoftYaHei、缺EA的Latin-collection→MicrosoftYaHei、cross-slot→MicrosoftYaHei、bare zh→HiraginoSansGB、缺字Arial→MicrosoftYaHei。explicit/empty matching EA/CS的SC/TC/ja/ar/he及Greek支持当前选择；LO差异不能替代PP语义。选中family与actual glyph fallback仍分开，bare zh和缺EA实验规则不宣称PP parity。

PP另存round1/2/3，各轮关闭、重新打开、导出PDF。独立ZIP/ElementTree顺实际presentation→slide→layout→master→theme读20页run拓扑/文本/lang/slots、local list fonts、minor generic/supplemental规则，三轮无selected声明变化；各页固定PyMuPDF scale栅格与初始完全相同。[三轮manifest](../testing/powerpoint-font-native-evidence.json)记录所有PPTX/PDF hash；实际ZIP修改一页EA为Wrong Font后同一reader检测变化且其余19页不变。临时native窗口和20页contact sheet已查看，CJK可见；generic Hebrew的mark/spacing差异保留，抽取文本不证明shaping。仅关闭本轮字体窗口，未操作下层已有Cycle窗口。

**任务落盘：** [8张执行卡](advanced-capability-todos.md#真实字体证据之后的执行卡)分开regular证据、同face SVG/fresh原生、mixed bidi、emoji、native优先级5差异、author-map编辑、source字体主题编辑、notes原生；同步ROADMAP/next-delivery及API局限。仅.5.1限定样本切片完成，.5/.6、字体父任务与总目标继续active；无编辑三轮不替代改字/样式/换主题验收。

对抗式审查五项：

1. **默认native字体不可见却误判逻辑错误/旧结果改绿**：实际图片、PDF实际PS与child-only Fontconfig对照；历史空白仍是失败观察，不全局安装/改配置。
2. **CSS名字/度量巧合冒充真实字体或TTC错face**：source/index/version/hash/cmap、提取前后完整HB glyph与position、CDP实际custom PS/glyphCount、DOM/Layout/HB误差和可见截图同时检查；未提供style/缺字/emoji均有located拒绝。
3. **合并夹具改变源规则或靠PowerPoint修复通过**：首份明确取消；正确版常规path/唯一master-layout IDs、关系闭合、actual slide/theme payload hash与原source一致。错误font实际ZIP负例通过同reader，未只改expected对象。
4. **LO或PP单机fallback被烘焙为源规则**：完整保留15/5差异，声明选择与实际coverage分开；本轮不改v2/default、不查询host字体到Core/Parser、不扩大writer。native优先级留新.6.1执行卡。
5. **绿测试或无编辑保存冒充全部字体/完整目标完成**：SVG、mixed bidi、emoji、主题/样式/notes编辑独立未勾选；HB孤立RTL不是Unicode bidi验收，selected声明/raster不是完整package byte preservation。所有完整父任务与总目标继续active。

最终验证：`pnpm build`、`pnpm typecheck`、`pnpm lint`、`pnpm test`全部退出0。Parser669、Core145、Layout137、Exporter52、SVG19、CLI11、dev-app16、visual27、browser bundles34、installed consumers5、script-font authoring跨包8、import integrations102及既有research/fixture/composition/copy/table组均通过。真实字体runner的8个正例和4个located负例/fallback断言、20页native选择与三轮独立ZIP/PDF oracle通过；没有为本轮研究增加CI对专有宿主字体的依赖。最后文档链接/typed examples与`git diff --check`复验通过。本轮无GUI删除、提交或发布，总目标未完成。

日志：`.tmp/real-font-final-build.log`、`.tmp/real-font-final-typecheck.log`、`.tmp/real-font-final-lint.log`、`.tmp/real-font-final-test.log`、`.tmp/real-font-final-docs.log`、`.tmp/real-font-native.log`、`.tmp/real-font-shaping-final.log`。本地native原件/PDF为`.tmp/powerpoint-font-probe`；fonts仅在`.tmp`，未提交字体二进制。下一步优先native选择差异定界与同face SVG，再推进源/author-map的原生改字、样式和主题编辑；完整notes与source writer仍按原任务准入。

## Paired PowerPoint font precedence controls — 2026-10-26

上一轮是实质进展，本轮继续IMP-05.1.3.3.2.6.1。新增[32成对source控制](../../scripts/research/font-native-cases.mjs)，给[native runner](../../scripts/inspect-real-font-native.mjs)增加`--precedence`与不调用native producer的`--prepare-only`，保持原20控制和历史LO manifest独立。原20组当前builder生成的slide/theme payload与已验证原件逐项SHA完全一致；未改生产API/字体规则，也没有安装字体或全局环境变更。

初版27组已通过PP原生打开/导出，发现ko Hangul仍替代；随后加入5个ko-KR与Han/alternate map配对，最终32页全部在PowerPoint原生打开成功、无修复提示，导出PDF。只有本轮临时文件被关闭，下层已有Cycle窗口未操作。最终[manifest](../testing/powerpoint-font-precedence-evidence.json)为**20匹配/12差异**，实际source文件在`.tmp/real-font-native/run-lPDVJa`，native输入/PDF为`.tmp/powerpoint-font-probe/font-precedence32.*`。32页contact sheet已查看，实际PDF traces无glyph zero；此项不代替完整shaping或原生编辑验收。

**修正上一轮解释：** 实际fc-query cmap显示Apple SD Gothic Neo覆盖本例全部Han与Hangul，Arial Unicode MS也覆盖；原先把韩语差异推测为覆盖问题不足。short`ko`主题Hangul用MalgunGothic，proper-region`ko-KR`同主题引用用AppleSDGothicNeo，空genericEA也一致；ko-KR Han direct/theme均用Apple，改Hang supplemental为HiraginoSansGB则命中改后family。shortko即使指定覆盖Han/Hangul的ArialUnicodeMS map仍选择其他字体。因此这是具名PowerPoint语言tag差异，不能改写source语言，也不说明BCP47短ko无效。微软MinorFont官方例子确认script=Hang，不以改script代码迎合应用。

**其他定界：** 覆盖字体作为Latin时，TC缺EA用ArialUnicodeMS、present+mn-ea用SongtiTC；Arabic/Hebrew缺/显式空CS用Arial、present+mn-cs用各自supplemental。v2缺槽的Latin-collection补充规则推断已有native parity反证，不再以“尚未测试”掩盖；现显式profile兼容不等于该推断native-correct。covered cross-slot ea=+mn-lt与等价literal均用HiraginoSansGB，支持当前具体槽语义；major SC/TC/ja refs、Hant替换/省略均按具名控制工作。barezh跟随Hans条目、替换Hans后用SongtiTC、无Hans则MicrosoftYaHei；Core保守ambiguous规则保留，显式zh-Hans/Hant命中预期face。

扩展[独立oracle](../../scripts/research/oracles/font-native-deck.py)：actual source text/lang/local slots、major/minor generic/supplemental、实际slide-layout-master-theme关系、payload hash和实际presentation slide顺序均验。新增[五实际ZIP负例](../../scripts/research/oracles/font-native-deck-checks.py)：错slot、lang、supplemental、重定向到另一个有效主题链、交换presentation顺序，故意更新transport/member hashes后仍须被semantic检查拒绝。最后一个负例先暴露旧oracle只信manifest页序而未查presentation的漏洞，已修正；全部5负例拒绝，原20和新32源控制在加强reader下通过。此修正只加强研究验证，不扩大任何生产reader/writer权限。

[实测文档](../testing/real-fonts.md#paired-native-precedence-controls)、ADR0049/API局限、ROADMAP与next-delivery/TODO同步：.6.1仍未完成，接续表格fontRef/notes与保守source-profile合同修正，必须覆盖双方言/provenance/candidate/scalar保存；不能把单机native fallback烘焙进Core或sourcefamily/lang。SVG、mixed bidi、emoji与native改字/样式/换主题继续原任务。完整目标保持active。

对抗式审查五项：

1. **把覆盖/语言/主题选择混为一谈**：actualcmap+direct/theme+ko/ko-KR+alternate覆盖map成对；主动更正覆盖推测，全部12差异保留，不把匹配率当成功率。
2. **为了native绿结果修改源或旧profile**：无生产规则改动；缺槽Latin补充规则明确已有反证，需新合同/兼容迁移和跨owner回归，不宣称现v2native完整。shortko/barezh应用差异不改写source。
3. **新夹具污染历史20控制**：独立数组、独立manifest，原20的40个slide/theme payload SHA逐项不变；prepare-only无native调用，所有实际PP文件均无repair接受。
4. **hash自证/关系闭合但指错主题/页序**：五个实际ZIP mutation故意重算hash；semantic reader查真实关系与presentation list，先抓到页序漏洞再修复，metadata cardinality/identity也显式比对。
5. **单轮selection冒充三轮编辑/完整目标**：本32控制只做native打开/PDF观察；上一轮20控制的无编辑三轮保存另存manifest，不挪用为新矩阵或native编辑验收。源style writer、表格/notes/native编辑全未扩权，父任务不勾选。

最终验证：`pnpm build`、`pnpm typecheck`、`pnpm lint`、`pnpm test`均退出0；Parser669、Core145、Layout137、Exporter52、SVG19、CLI11、dev-app16、visual27、browser bundles34、installed consumers5、script-font跨包8、import integrations102及既有research/fixture/composition/copy/table组通过。32正例source/actualPDF与5实际ZIP负例、旧20源payload兼容检查通过；未知CLI flag明确退出1。最后文档typed examples/links与`git diff --check`复验通过。没有GUI删除、提交、发布或生产API改动，完整目标未完成。

日志：`.tmp/font-precedence-build.log`、`.tmp/font-precedence-typecheck.log`、`.tmp/font-precedence-lint.log`、`.tmp/font-precedence-test.log`、`.tmp/font-precedence-docs.log`、`.tmp/font-precedence-negative-final.log`、`.tmp/font-precedence-prepare.log`、`.tmp/font-precedence-invalid-cli.log`。后续优先保守source字体合同与表格/notes/native对照，继而同face SVG和原生编辑准入；当前32控制仅定界选择，不把实验推断当完整幻灯片能力。

补充prepare-only隔离检查：给runner一个明确不存在的native executable路径，仍退出0并生成32source控制，证明该模式无需/不调用soffice；本地日志`.tmp/font-precedence-no-native.log`，source目录`.tmp/real-font-native/run-kwk41y`。正式PP evidence仍引用run-lPDVJa，不将未做native的prepare结果替换已观察证据。

## Declared-slot supplemental source profile — 2026-10-26

上一轮通过32组native控制明确了v2缺槽Latin字典推断的反证，是实质进展。本轮推进IMP-05.1.3.3.2.6.1的source合同修正，交付[ADR0051](../architecture/decisions/0051-declared-slot-supplemental-projection.md)、[API/typed example](../api/declared-slot-font-import.md)、[独立证据](../testing/declared-slot-font-import.md)。新增显式`textFontProfile: "script-families-v3"`，default/v1/v2行为兼容，不将原生替代或语言tag写回source，不宣称通用native字体解析。

v3复用既有37-key source map解析、theme/collection选择与原生field cascade，只改最终选择：script须通过自己的effective native bucket；缺省EA/CS不再借Latin字典。对应source条目仍逐key记录unsupported、无value、实际字体node/source及Latinselector依赖和target，并通过所属drawing/notes/table的bounded finding sink解释边界。继承存在的slot继续有效；matching theme refs的generic为空仍可映射有效具体script；literal/cross-slot不获map；explicit table collection拥有bucket choice且generic缺失block与自身map分开。未按当前字符/lang/宿主字体烘焙选择，未新增Core字段/包/依赖。

绘图、表格、notes、empty insertion共用finalization，private maps仍在报告前移除；新unsupported properties/dependencies/findings沿原quota，notes主题工作保持原budget。bytes/file首次await前capture、actual candidate重投影保留v3，完整canonical snapshot一致而不maskfont/map；source scalar可跨script改字。混入font/map/lang/style编辑仍原子blocked，restore/retry及无save profile权限保持原guard。

## Actual SVG font paths and text boundary corrections — 2026-10-26

上一轮v3实现/独立source证据属于实质推进。本轮推进IMP-05.1.3.3.2.5.2的真实SVG切片，交付[ADR0052](../architecture/decisions/0052-svg-text-boundaries-and-font-names.md)、[API边界](../api/svg-renderer.md)、[字体SVG证据](../testing/real-font-svg.md)与[runner](../../scripts/inspect-real-font-svg.mjs)。没有新增公共选项、类型、依赖、字体安装或source writer权限，未用HTML-only specimen替代SVG验证。

**真实SVG：** 读取原shaping实验的source/extracted face并核SHA，用7具名regular字体的8字符串分别走actual native `<text>/<tspan>`与两run `foreignObject`（小号Arial prefix+样本），共16样本。CDP actual custom PS/glyphCount、SVG text/range advance对原face HB小于0.05px、无warning/禁网、作者runs不变均验。初版原名字实验16对PNG一致且已查看图片，但同名宿主字体可能掩盖image隔离，所以最终换成hash-derived unique aliases，并在image自己的SVG内由harness注入exact bytes。Renderer本身仍无字体I/O或embedding API。

aliases暴露native font-family未quote导致数字名称部分解释为CSS语法的真实错误；原`sc/native`实际PS检查拒绝。shared CSS family encoder由原HTML helper移入escaping模块，native text/chart和XHTML一致使用quoted literal name。另以带逗号/引号的pinned alias验证text/chart advances，并把相同正确registered face的DOM属性改回旧unquoted值，负例必须偏离；一度把FontFace constructor的literal family参数误当CSS表达式预quote，诊断registered值后改回raw family，未放宽目标断言。Node安装消费者与现有script-map parity按CSS family属性语义更新读取，保留原实际family期望。

最终[manifest](../testing/real-font-svg-evidence.json)源artifact为`.tmp/real-font-svg/run-b7LS7g`：16样本均为exact custom face、visible dark ink，inline与embedded-image PNG逐像素一致；移除image-local字体后Latin alias画面不同，证明不是继承外层document FontFace或偶遇宿主同名字体。独立[PNG oracle](../../scripts/research/oracles/real-font-svg.py)检查尺寸/ink/bounds/exactpixels并记录PNG hash；[实际PNG破坏检查](../../scripts/research/oracles/real-font-svg-checks.py)白图、改1像素、改尺寸均拒绝。原始与最终alias contact sheet均已查看，Han/Hangul/Arabic/marks/Hebrew/Latin accent可见；末尾无embedding控制字体不同。只证明具名Chromium，不延伸Office/Firefox/WebKit或foreignObject通用移植性。

**由控制暴露并修正的运行时问题：** 原native `wrap:false`仍输出6行，native标点/空白tokenizer把连续/末尾换行吞掉，并在强制窄wrap时把`á`、`👩🏽‍💻`、`🇨🇳`拆成8行。现在实际wrap flag禁止softbreak、CRLF/CR/LF分别保留、重复/末尾空行计入vertical cursor；author whitespace保留，soft-wrap分隔空白在新行省略。joined paragraph先Intl grapheme segment再映回selected-family pieces，宽于frame的cluster可溢出但不可拆分；nonspacing/enclosing marks/join controls不另加估算advance。无Intl/Segmenter时用安全escaped XHTML，非import crash。宽度与baseline仍是既有heuristic，不以Intl冒充shaper/Unicode line-break/bidi引擎。

mixed XHTML另有实际反例：`M\n\n`段和empty段高度为60/0，而browser measurement需要90/30。新增最后effective style的aria-hidden零宽display placeholder，只处理empty/final-empty line，保持authored span[lang]文本和Core输入不变。新browser回归验证正确heights与provider一致。既有native x/y/baseline断言保持，smoke只增加显式whitespace属性期望；未替换任何视觉baseline。

新增[6组浏览器回归](../../packages/svg-renderer/test/measurement.spec.mjs)：pinned no-wrap与空白Canvas/XHTML一致、连续CRLF/CR/LF与末尾空行位置、跨family fragment字素完整、缺Segmenter安全fallback/escaping、numeric/comma/quote family在text/chart的actual advances和旧raw属性反例、mixed empty段与末尾空行高度对measurement。前三组先全部红测；真实alias检查与空HTML高度也先拒绝后修正。结构上保护emoji cluster不等于emoji glyph/shaping准入。

对抗式审查五项：

1. **量到宽度但字形不可见/错字体同度量**：actual custom PS+glyphCount、原/提取face SHA、HB advance、PNG dark ink与可见截图并用；不把CSS声明或DOM text存在当pass。
2. **inline/image共享字体或宿主同名掩盖embedding无效**：unique aliases排除宿主family，isolated image必须自带font bytes；无embedding同alias画面必须不同。三实际PNG mutation检验oracle，不只修改expected数据。
3. **nowrap/换行修复又拆cluster或破坏font-fragment样式**：joined文本先分字素、UTF16 offset线性映回原runs；强制width1的组合/ZWJ/旗帜反例、硬换行/末尾empty与原baseline/长段wrap同时验证。Intl缺失直接fallback，无新包/全局字体状态。
4. **字体转义仍把literal当CSS列表/引号注册反例自证**：共用原HTML CSS+XML encoder到text/chart；correct raw FontFace registration下对照old raw属性实际失配，保留literal family与源style，不用改alias逃避数字词错误。
5. **新SVG样本宣称完整native/font父任务**：此轮无PowerPoint操作/新native appearance认证，.5.2的fresh-PPTX部分、mixed bidi、emoji与native表格/notes/编辑仍开放；harness embedding非public API，library仍不读字体文件。全目标继续active。

3. **PDF资源同名合并掩盖错误、空格忽略变成验收漏洞**：BaseFont/FontDescriptor/trace联合核对，逐字符非空白font+XML完整空白+可见栅格共同使用；单资源与全部资源改名控制分开。
4. **首次theme规范化或pPr移除被当作无变化**：raw hash/方案/链分别记录并用具名native baseline约束，pPr None单列；只声称阶段内三轮raw与栅格稳定，不声称原始source字节或不同阶段布局不变。
5. **六组配对混合段被扩大为完整七脚本/bidi能力**：当前.6.2.3和父项仍开放，下一步同段完整七脚本组合；native表格fontRef/notes与mixed-bidi/marks/emoji各按原卡，不以source text或glyph0=0证明所有轮廓/顺序正确。

最终build/typecheck/lint、10组字体跨包、72反例/17精确no-op和既有SC/五脚本oracle退出0；文档与diff检查通过。未声称重跑完整runtime全套；日志`.tmp/real-font-mixed.log`、`.tmp/real-font-mixed-{build,typecheck,lint,test}.log`、`.tmp/real-font-mixed-{sc,multi}-regression.log`。总目标继续active。

55个notes/部件ZIP/PDF加18个ordinary-slide PDF实际破坏全拒绝，共73份文件带SHA/原因。涵盖源text/lang/style/local/master/theme/override/layout/default kern/真实页序、字体资源、允许位移区域的header遮白、普通slide嵌入字体字节变化、额外几何/墨迹和后轮栅格。原无编辑/追加/局部样式oracle准入规则不变。普通slide证据绑定主manifest hash，不能把后补PDF输出当新的保存三轮。

验证：build/typecheck、三组扩充source回归、主native runner/独立反例、ordinary-slide runner/18反例、Node脚本语法通过；lint/边界/文档和diff检查另已运行。日志.tmp/native-notes-themes-{build,typecheck,evidence,lint}.log、.tmp/native-notes-theme-slides-evidence.log、.tmp/notes-distinct-override-source-tests.log。没有重跑/宣称完整workspace runtime套件。无生产API/reader/writer权限变更，无提交或发布。

反向审查五项：不能以单master基数正确推native准入——shared repair和三个单变量控制保留；不能以同文件号猜域或override——native重排/重命名沿实际关系及标签核；不能把notes主题不变写成全页不变——header末字A和普通slide两类位移明确，整页反向平移只作内存诊断；不能把可提取/零advance当可见——真实嵌入glyph bounds、墨迹、字体程序bytes和整页栅格及遮白反例；不能把headless/复制baseline/后补普通PDF冒充native轮次——180主题Notes/90新基线/30首次与240ordinary及45候选分列。主题具名子卡完成，.6.4.2.5汇总、16字体差异、shared准入限制、完整notes页/slide-image、source style writer及总体目标继续开放。

## 2026-10-27 raw JPEG crop mapping

Previous turn strengthened actual installed DOM module-graph evidence. Layout resolves contain/cover using registered dimensions before asset resolution; replacing JPEG bytes later does not recompute geometry. Added explicit pure `orientJpegCrop` to exporter default, browser, Node and DOM entries. It maps a complete raw-pixel-coordinate crop to the same region after EXIF orientation 1–8, rejects nonfinite, out-of-range and empty regions or invalid orientation, and leaves inputs untouched. It does not decide a frame, swap dimensions, rewrite a presentation, retain screen orientation, or infer whether a crop is already oriented. Callers must register oriented dimensions and choose their own fit/frame policy.

Tests check asymmetric edge mappings and inverse orientations, zero and invalid boundaries, entry parity and detached output. Chromium checks each of the eight mappings against the actual transformed pixels, while an integration test confirms a mapped orientation-6 crop reaches Layout and the serialized `a:srcRect` and that normalized PNG bytes are packaged. ## 2026-10-27 per-image replacement plan

Previous turn established a named native crop-handle edit/import/preview observation. Core now provides pure `planImageReplacement` with explicit preserve, stretch, contain, cover or caller-specified size policies. It returns deeply frozen detached replacement and previous image snapshots for existing element replacement/undo; it does not mutate assets or the document, register a target, infer pixel-to-slide size, change other users of a shared asset, remove assets or write imported package parts. Preserve means crop fractions and authored frame, not identical content or resolved visible geometry. Contain/cover validate target/frame dimensions and numerical aspect safety; size requires positive finite presentation-unit dimensions.

Core tests cover policies, shared-use isolation, snapshots/undo, invalid fields, crop and dimension failures, extreme aspect underflow, and atomic no-mutation. A package integration test retargets one shared image from a 2×3 PNG to a 3×2 JPEG, verifies cover layout and PPTX crop serialization, two independent media resources and restoration. An initial exact floating-point equality and incorrect media extension in the test were corrected. Browser installed consumer executes the public plan, and installed TypeScript checks its contract. Core tests 241/241, exporter 126/126, installed consumers 6/6, build, typecheck and lint passed; build has a large-chunk advisory.

Adversarial review checked shared identity and stale undo, declaration versus decoded image dimensions, normalized versus raw EXIF, numerical limits, authored versus resolved visible frame, and separation from preservation-aware package writing. Native image replacement, general source crop policies, transparency, unknown references, importer edit-aware save and full backlog remain open.

## 2026-10-27 resolved visible-frame replacement plan

Previous turn added transparent replacement cross-package evidence, while source-preserving image writes remained blocked. It rejects unknown IDs, unresolved contain/cover and zero-area frames. ## 2026-10-27 composed visible-frame replacement

Previous turn added default-layout visible-frame replacement planning. The Layout API now accepts an optional explicit composition snapshot, runs it before image fitting and uses the resulting top-level image frame. Any resulting layout diagnostic blocks the plan, so failed rules or other diagnostics are not silently ignored. It reads the composition option once, clones it before reading the document and preserves authored undo state. Other Layout options, pagination, nested objects and master/layout-owned images remain unsupported. A plan captures geometry only; reapplying the same composition after committing can move or resize the image again.

Tests cover composed alignment followed by contain fitting, detached rules, single-read capture, failed/missing targets, unsupported options, unchanged document and undo. Export integration checks a composed resolved frame in generated PPTX with no implicit recomposition. Installed browser/type consumers exercise the option. Layout tests 147/147, exporter tests 128/128, installed consumers 6/6, build, typecheck and lint passed; build emitted the existing large-chunk advisory. Adversarial review examined double application, partial composition, stale plan, unrelated diagnostics, numerical geometry and undo of authored versus resolved state. Native replacement, further layout stages, nested/master ownership and source-preserving image writeback remain open; full backlog remains active.


## 2026-10-27 nested visible-frame image replacement

Previous turn added composition-aware visible-frame planning for top-level images. Layout now also exposes `planNestedVisibleImageReplacement` for a slide-owned group subtree. It resolves the selected image in its immediate parent's local coordinates and returns detached deeply frozen whole-root-group replacement and previous snapshots for the slide's existing atomic `replaceElement`. Siblings, group IDs, transforms and shared asset uses remain intact. Optional composition may place the child; if a composed ancestor group frame or transform differs from default layout, the operation rejects rather than implying that the uncommitted parent movement was preserved. Other sibling composition outputs are not included, and applying composition again may move content. No source-package edit permission is added.

Layout tests cover two group levels, parent scaling/rotation/flip, local contain geometry, shared sibling isolation, detached snapshots and undo, local composition and ancestor-change refusal, missing identity/assets and unresolved fit. Cross-package tests verify fresh PPTX export, content reimport, nested frame, exact independent media and undo. Installed browser/type consumers cover the entry. Layout tests 150/150, cross-package parity 2/2, installed consumers 6/6, build, typecheck and lint passed; build retains a large-chunk advisory. Adversarial review checked local vs slide-space bounds, ancestor changes, sibling preservation, stale root snapshots, group identity/timeline validation through existing commit, and distinction between fresh export and source-preserving writeback. Master/layout-owned groups, coupled ancestor transformations, image replacement in imported source packages and full backlog remain open.


## 2026-10-27 shared master/layout visible image replacement

The change is shared by every page displaying that definition, not a slide-local override. Optional composition and ancestor-frame guards match nested slide planning; altered sibling geometry is not implicitly committed. Unresolved or empty frames, wrong owner/image/asset and diagnostic-producing composition reject. No source-package preservation writing is implied.

Layout tests cover direct master artwork, nested layout artwork, definition metadata and visibility, shared references, owner isolation, local composition, ancestor movement rejection, failure atomicity and undo. Export integration follows both slide → layout → master relationships, confirms a single master picture references the new media and no per-slide picture copies, checks its resolved frame and source bytes. This is not a chart-cache, GUI, PowerPoint or general compatibility test; the parent chart gates remain open.

Fresh chart name/category export now encodes XML-forbidden control characters and BMP noncharacters using Xstring escapes, alongside literal escape protection; isolated surrogates continue to fail explicitly. Integration checks exercise all ASCII controls, both BMP noncharacters, literal escape-looking text, emoji, independent cache/worksheet inspection, reimport and numeric binding. The focused chart suite passed 7 tests, exporter suite 170 tests, and build, typecheck and lint passed. Four exploratory rich-text splits did not prevent the previously recorded Calc overlapping-literal loss; those local probes do not establish a compatible alternative or native behavior for controls.

SVG chart previews now replace XML-forbidden displayed title/category/legend characters with U+FFFD and emit one located `chart-text-xml-replacement` warning per affected chart; carriage returns use an XML character reference. The source document stays unchanged. Independent XML parsing verifies a preview containing all ASCII controls and BMP noncharacters; additional tests cover pie/bar labels, hidden text, emoji, deterministic warnings and unchanged input. The chart integration suite passed 7 tests, the renderer suite passed 33 tests, and build, typecheck and lint passed. Other SVG text owners remain outside this change.

The retained notes-slide-image captures were independently rechecked without modifying their manifests: 15 source pages, 45 original and 48 copied-slide reopens with six negative controls, and B/override's 45 first-open and 135 reopened pages with ten negative controls matched the recorded results. These are rechecks, not new native operations. The fresh notes-page DOM oracle now also enforces vertical centering and maximal fit, rejecting shifted and undersized image controls across the four existing slide sizes. The notes-page and native-font browser tests and lint passed. The original empty-style failure remains recorded; full notes field/artwork and source-style gates remain open.

Fresh PPTX generation now validates each rounded slide dimension against the PresentationML 914400–51206400 EMU range before asset resolution, instead of emitting a package that the parser rejects. The default notes master fits its slide image to the rounded dimensions actually written to `p:sldSz`, avoiding a fractional-boundary aspect mismatch. Boundary and out-of-range controls include both orientations, Node/browser parity, independent notes geometry, parser reinspection and an asset resolver that must not run; existing default byte baselines remain unchanged. Full `pnpm test` passed, including 157 import/browser tests, and the exporter suite passed 171 tests; typecheck, lint and diff checks passed. This deliberately changes fresh-export behavior for previously accepted invalid sizes and does not change Core or SVG admission. Native application behavior at the new boundary inputs remains unverified.

Three boundary-size fresh two-slide decks were separately resaved three times with LibreOfficeDev Impress in headless mode. The [retained evidence](../testing/notes-size-libreoffice-evidence.json) and independent ZIP/XML inventory verify 12 actual files, slide dimensions, notes bodies, page order and bidirectional relationships. The app rewrote the master slide-image frame and added local slide-image frames; each later save decreased local width and height by 360 EMU while positions remained fixed. Four forged-manifest cases and three rehashed corrupted PPTX files are rejected. No notes PDF, repair-dialog, UI or PowerPoint behavior is established; full notes gates stay open.

All 12 retained boundary files now have [actual-file parser coverage](../../scripts/notes-size-libreoffice-parser.test.mjs): source inventory reproduces master and local image frames, page/placeholder identities and the unresolved geometry policy; Node/ESM/global reports and original-byte no-op agree. From each third LibreOffice resave, three further source-preserving notes-character edits retain both local frames and all other payloads; an independent ZIP verifier checks the exact single text replacement and rejects damaged geometry or neighboring notes. The optional actual-file tests passed 2/2 and lint passed. These are parser/save checks against existing files, not additional native application rounds or geometry write support.

SVG slide serialization now replaces remaining XML-forbidden characters with U+FFFD and emits a slide-scoped `svg-xml-character-replacement` warning; chart text retains its more specific warning. It also preserves escaped carriage returns through XML character references and applies byte budgets to the final SVG. Tests cover text and attributes, selection, unchanged input, UTF-8 byte limits, emoji, malformed surrogate replacement, independent XML parsers and Node/ESM/global parity. Renderer tests passed 35/35, parity 2/2 and Chromium visual tests 35/35; typecheck and lint passed. This provides valid SVG serialization with explicit display loss; it does not complete the broader typography or portable-SVG tasks.

For AUT-01.2.1, SVG list markers no longer clamp alphabetic starts at Z: shared Core `formatListMarker` now supplies the same base-26 sequence to SVG and browser measurement, with deterministic validation and a located warning for starts above 27 because native conventions differ. Core rejects unsafe integers; fresh PPTX export rejects emitted starts outside the OOXML 1–32767 range for text, shapes, tables and notes. An isolated [LibreOfficeDev PDF observation](../testing/alphabetic-numbering-libreoffice-evidence.json) extracted `z, aa, ab, az, ba, bb` at 26/27/28/52/53/54, whereas Microsoft's specification example says 53 is `aaa`; no PowerPoint behavior or interactive numbering is claimed. Core/Layout/Exporter/Renderer suites passed 384/169/172/36 tests, focused Node/ESM/global and evidence tests passed, 35 Chromium visual tests passed, and the installed-package suite passed 57 tests. Typecheck, lint and diff checks passed. Cross-paragraph continuation, nesting, restart, tabs and the parent task remain open.

AUT-01.2.1 now also supports explicit adjacent same-style `startAt: "continue"` in authored paragraph arrays. Core resolves it to numeric IR, recalculates after edits, preserves origin information and diagnoses invalid dependencies; export warns that the dependency is materialized. Focused tests cover text, shapes, tables, notes, groups, masters, layouts and inherited placeholders, the OOXML output, import and Node/browser parity. Core/Layout/Exporter/Renderer/Parser suites passed 388/169/172/36/1086 tests, Chromium visual tests passed 35, and installed-package tests passed 57. Typecheck, lint and diff checks passed. This is partial: native continuation editing, list levels, tabs, narrow-frame and PowerPoint behavior remain unverified; the task remains open.

Tab-stop investigation identified three distinct paths: the SVG native-text approximation treats tabs as whitespace, the XHTML/browser measurement paths use CSS layout, and the fresh exporter writes tab characters without authored paragraph tab stops. SVG now emits `text-tab-layout-unsupported` for affected text bodies, including table cells; this diagnostic does not implement tabs. [Microsoft's DrawingML tab-stop description](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/dotnet/api/documentformat.openxml.drawing.tabstop?view=openxml-3.0.1) describes ordered paragraph stops, relative positions and alignment, while omitted values are application-dependent. The native editing, default-stop and alignment behavior still needs independent evidence. Renderer tests passed 37, Chromium visual tests passed 35, Node/ESM/global parity passed, and typecheck, lint and diff checks passed.

Core now carries optional paragraph tab stops through presets, placeholder resets, table styles and normalized IR; fresh PPTX emits ordered `a:tabLst` entries for left, center, right and decimal alignment. Validation rejects malformed entries, invalid coordinate range, and positions that collide after EMU rounding. Focused tests cover inheritance, empty overrides, origin tracking, table rules, boundary values and slide/notes XML, with Node/ESM/global export parity. The Core/Layout/Exporter/Renderer/Parser suites passed 391/169/172/37/1086 tests, alongside later focused boundary/reset cases, 35 browser visual tests and 57 installed-package tests; typecheck, lint and diff checks passed. This is not full tab support: preview and measurement do not apply stops, fresh import does not recover them, and native alignment, default stops and editing are unverified.

Content import now maps explicit ordered tab lists of up to 256 stops through the paragraph style cascade, including empty local overrides, and records each stop as a budgeted dependency. Missing application-default fields, unsupported alignments and nonincreasing or oversized lists block older declarations; malformed numeric coordinates reject import. Focused tests cover strict/transitional input, source provenance, exact dependency limits, original-byte no-op save, generated slide/notes/table reimport and Node/browser import parity. The Parser suite passed 1090 tests, installed-package tests passed 57, and typecheck, lint and diff checks passed. No source-preserving tab-stop edit profile or native visual proof is claimed; preview and measurement remain open.

An isolated [LibreOfficeDev tab-stop PDF observation](../testing/tab-stops-libreoffice.md) compared the generated four-alignment deck, a same-run variant and a no-stop control. All measured sample words began at 131.981 pt in all three variants despite explicit stops at 340 pt in the first two; this is a material compatibility concern, not proof of PowerPoint behavior. Independent verification reopens each retained ZIP and PDF, checks hashes, declarations, run boundaries and text geometry, and rejects tampered evidence. Native PowerPoint comparison and editing remain open; the Mac reported locked during this attempt.

The follow-up [six-variant attribute isolation](../testing/tab-stop-indent-libreoffice-evidence.json) found that removing the explicit zero `indent` enables the four tab alignments in the tested LibreOfficeDev conversion; removing `marL`, `algn`, or `buNone`, or changing `-0` to `0`, does not. The independent verifier compares every package part and the exact allowed slide change before remeasuring the PDFs; tamper tests passed. Export now warns for explicit stops with tabs and zero first-line indent, retaining the zero because dropping it could change inherited styling. Exporter tests passed 172, focused Node/browser and evidence tests passed 8, and typecheck, lint and diff checks passed. Nonzero indentation and PowerPoint behavior remain unverified.

The browser font-backed measurement adapter now rejects tabbed text rather than returning CSS tab expansion as though it were native tab-stop geometry. Layout reports a located `text-measurement-failed` and omits measurements for the affected body; other custom providers keep their own contracts. Pinned-font browser tests cover explicit/default/empty stops, mixed runs, hard breaks, text, shape and table owners, a no-tab control and a custom provider. Layout passed 169 tests, Chromium passed 36 visual/measurement tests and installed-package tests passed 57; typecheck, lint and diff checks passed. This is an explicit behavioral change, not an implementation of tab-stop layout; fitting tabbed text still requires an appropriate provider.

Browser measurement now resolves an explicit-stop subset: printable ASCII, no-wrap, left paragraph alignment, zero indent/hanging and no list marker, with up to 256 stops. It uses loaded font advances for left/center/right/period-decimal alignment, mixed runs and consecutive tabs; missing stops, overlapping segments and unsupported contexts fail instead of returning partial geometry. Chromium checks compare positions with independently measured Canvas advances, including 256-stop and mixed-size cases. Layout passed 169 tests, the full browser suite passed 37 plus the updated focused 25-test suite, installed-package tests passed 57, and typecheck, lint and diff checks passed. SVG, wrapping, native Office fidelity and general tab semantics remain open.

The browser subset now handles nonnegative paragraph indent and hanging up to that indent: stops use the left-margin coordinate and hanging moves the first-line start. Four [synthetic ODF controls](../testing/tab-stop-paragraph-libreoffice-evidence.json) provide one LibreOfficeDev PDF observation each; their verifier checks ODF styles, unrelated package content, PDF word geometry and decimal characters. A browser test exposed and fixed inherited CSS `text-indent` on inline fragments before passing mixed-size, zero-position, indent and hanging controls against Canvas advances. Layout passed 169 tests, Chromium passed 37 full and 25 focused tests, installed-package tests passed 57, and the three evidence tests plus typecheck/lint/diff checks passed. These controls do not establish PowerPoint behavior; wrapping, list markers, general Unicode and SVG placement remain open.

The bounded browser tab profile now admits Unicode Han alongside printable ASCII. Pinned Latin and CJK font controls test left/right/decimal stops, mixed font slots and run boundaries against independent Canvas advances; direct-provider negative controls reject Hebrew, Arabic, kana, combining marks, emoji and variation selectors without returning geometry. Layout passed 169 tests, Chromium passed 37 full and 25 focused tests, installed-package tests passed 57, and typecheck/lint/diff checks passed. This is not a general Unicode shaping/bidi implementation, native Office equivalence, or SVG tab rendering.

The browser profile now handles explicit CR/LF breaks in tabbed paragraphs, restarting stop selection on each line and treating CRLF across run boundaries as one break. Empty lines, final empty lines, hanging-indent changes after the first line, and styled empty/tab/break runs have pinned-font tests; a 4096-control exact boundary and 4097 rejection bound DOM work. Metrics are read before applying tab margins to avoid a synchronous reflow per tab. Layout passed 169 tests, Chromium passed 39 full and 27 focused tests, installed-package tests passed 57, and typecheck/lint/diff checks passed. Automatic wrapping, line-ending tabs, native Office behavior and SVG tab placement remain open.

The bounded no-wrap tab profile now admits the documented precomposed hiragana/katakana and Hangul ranges. A separately licensed, hash-pinned Noto KR subset was generated twice to identical bytes; pinned-font browser tests compare Japanese/Korean mixed-run left/right/decimal positions against Canvas, and reject decomposed Jamo/kana, bidi text and all combining marks, including a Han-script mark. Layout passed 169 tests, Chromium passed 39, installed-package tests passed 57, and typecheck/lint/diff checks passed. This is not general grapheme shaping, bidi or PowerPoint fidelity.

A [48-case browser/LibreOffice comparison](../testing/tab-stop-browser-libreoffice-evidence.json) checked four explicit-stop alignments under four ODF paragraph-indent controls and three strings. The source requests Arial, but PDF font inspection identifies Liberation Sans regular/bold. Chromium used registered same-PostScript-name LibreOffice-bundled candidates, verified on CDP controls; HarfBuzz candidate advances differed from PDF widths by at most 0.121 pt and browser right edges by at most 0.146 pt. The verifier rechecks retained PDF/ODF source, font-file hashes, HarfBuzz and recorded arithmetic; four evidence tests, including tampering, passed. PDF font byte identity, PowerPoint, native editing, wrapping and bidi remain unproven.

Browser explicit-tab measurement now accepts the `•` and `arabicPeriod` list-marker subset with a nonempty first resolved run and verified marker font. The marker occupies at least the hanging width; longer markers occupy their measured width, and this list profile uses the content-frame left edge as the tab origin. A [36-slide LibreOfficeDev control](../testing/tab-list-libreoffice.md) checks marker/text positions and all four alignments for three marker/indent variants; its browser right-edge comparison differs by at most 0.139 pt. The PDF's symbol marker uses OpenSymbol while the browser uses its first-run font, so marker-font identity is not claimed. Canvas boundary tests cover long markers, stop selection, coverage failure and hard breaks; unsupported marker types reject. Layout passed 169 tests, Chromium passed 40, installed-package tests passed 57, five evidence/tamper tests passed, and typecheck/lint/diff checks passed. SVG, native Office, general markers and wrapping remain open.

The browser tab profile now also accepts wrapping-enabled frames when every explicit line fits the actual content width. It lays out those lines without soft wrapping and checks the final DOM edge after all tab positions are applied; any line requiring automatic wrapping rejects without a partial result. Pinned-font browser controls cover half-point fit/overflow boundaries, mixed ordinary/tabbed paragraphs, lists, shape text, default-wrapping table cells and a narrow table; no-wrap overflow behavior remains separate. Layout passed 169 tests, Chromium passed 41, installed-package tests passed 57, and typecheck/lint/diff checks passed. Actual automatic wrapping, SVG tab placement and PowerPoint verification remain open.

## Current worktree regression — 2026-10-31

`pnpm test` completed with exit 0; the final import integration stage passed 165 tests. The local full log is `.tmp/current-continuation-full-test.log`. The operation-evidence catalog and its tests changed while the suite was running, so the final nine catalog tests and `pnpm lint` were rerun separately after those edits. The optional local fixture check verified 19 distinct input files and all 29 catalog records; three connector evidence verifiers separately checked their retained source and output files. This is automated verification of the present worktree and the named observations, not a release, complete native matrix or acceptance of unchecked backlog tasks.

## Animation playback observation protocol — ADV-06.2.1.1

The [protocol](../testing/animation-playback-protocol.md) binds the four existing timing specimens to their recorded source hash and identifies per-effect hypotheses, application/version, capture and edit/reopen procedures, coordinate questions and existing headless rewrites. The retained source, headless output and PDF hashes were independently checked against the existing manifest; `pnpm lint` and `git diff --check` passed. Adversarial review separated source conditions from observed triggers, first/last frames from intermediate motion, headless conversion from actual playback, local artifacts from distributable fixtures, and regenerated bytes from the recorded hash. No playback was performed; ADV-06.2.1.2 and its parent remain open.

Subsequently, PowerPoint 16.104/macOS 26.5 opened that same hashed source and a manual slideshow inspection observed all four effects. The initial fade-in text was already visible; rotation, fading and path motion were seen after right-arrow triggers, and left-arrow reset the path on its page. Details and limitations are in the protocol. The transient UI snapshots were not retained as independent artifacts, no edit/save/reopen was performed, and LibreOffice GUI was not tested; .1.2 remains open. `pnpm lint` and `git diff --check` passed after the update.

On a separate source copy, PowerPoint changed the first effect's duration from 0.5 to 2 seconds and appended target text; after save/close/reopen, the first slide still showed a fade after a right-arrow. The [independent ZIP/XML oracle](../../scripts/research/oracles/animation-powerpoint-edit.py) verifies the saved target, 2000 ms, text and the other three behavior parameters; source, duration, target and text negative controls reject. The [protocol](../testing/animation-playback-protocol.md#powerpoint-fade-in-edit-and-reopen) records both hashes and the lack of retained video. This one edit does not close the four-effect/application playback matrix.

PowerPoint then saved a separate four-edit copy: all four target texts and durations were changed and the file was closed and reopened. Manual slideshow snapshots observed fade-in, rotation and path movement; the edited fade-out's final state was not independently captured. The oracle's `--all` mode checked the four target IDs, texts, 2000 ms durations and effect attributes and rejected the prior partial edit plus four actual corrupted ZIP controls. [Details and hash](../testing/animation-playback-protocol.md#four-edited-effects-in-powerpoint) distinguish XML evidence from UI observation; recording, LibreOffice GUI and full re-entry remain open.

The same four-edit copy was inspected again in PowerPoint: fade-out reached an invisible state, same-page left-arrow restored it, and returning from the next page initially retained the invisible state. Forward re-entry from the previous page restored the initial visible state. These observations have no retained video. The oracle now compares all timing-tree names, attributes, text and child order against the original, allowing only the expected duration changes; both edit files pass, while event/restart/fill/path-edit-mode mutations fail. This is semantic XML evidence, not a playback or whole-package equivalence claim.

The [animation integration suite](../../scripts/animation-mapping-parity.test.mjs) now combines an unknown foreign behavior and an inactive MCE character-range target in one timing tree. It checks that mixing a target text edit with an unrelated page edit blocks the whole plan, restoring the target permits retry, and every unchanged package part including the entire timing-owner slide remains byte-identical. All three focused integration tests pass after the workspace build; build, typecheck, lint and diff checks pass (build reports the existing large-chunk advisory). Adversarial review covered inactive versus selected branches, same-target versus unrelated-page edits, mixed-plan atomicity, exact preservation of the source part, and retry after restoration. This does not admit unknown timing or close ADV-06.2.1.3.

A further same-slide fixture combines a whole-shape and a character-range reference to one owner, a dangling ID and an unknown behavior. The public inventory locates the two owner references and reports the missing ID; mixed owner/bystander edits block, and restoring the owner permits the bystander save. The saved slide equals the original with exactly the intended text replacement, and all other parts remain byte-identical. Four focused integration tests, lint and diff checks pass. Review checked source identity versus unresolved targets, same-page isolation, blocked-plan retry, and the limits of one synthetic unknown behavior. Effect admission and the parent task remain open.

The native-edit XML oracle used Python assertions, which disappear under `python -O`; those checks now raise explicit errors. Its [optional artifact regression](../../scripts/research/oracles/test-animation-powerpoint-edit.py) accepted the real source/four-edit pair and rejected six actual mutated packages for their intended reasons under both normal and optimized Python. The files remain local and this strengthens evidence verification only, not playback admission. The installed LibreOffice is the headless build; attempting to open its app through the native UI timed out, so no LibreOffice GUI result is claimed.

The operation catalog previously called the named PowerPoint timing playback untested despite the later manual observation. It now records that original input as partial and separately records the edited input's partial replay, with fixture hashes and an explicit no-recording limitation in the [manifest](../testing/animation-powerpoint-observation-evidence.json). Both retain unknown capability; neither represents a playback acceptance or other application/version. The generated table comes from the catalog, not an independent result. Nine catalog tests pass, optional local verification checked 21 distinct retained files and all 32 records, and lint/diff checks pass. Review distinguished the original input from the edited file, partial observation from capability admission, fixture hash from UI provenance, and local artifacts from distributable fixtures.

The oracle regression now also runs against synthetic source/edited packages generated during the portable animation integration suite. It verifies the positive control and six corrupted packages under both Python modes without access to locally retained PowerPoint artifacts. The named native artifacts were separately rechecked; synthetic success does not establish native UI provenance. The parent backlog entry now reflects the partial manual observation instead of saying no playback was observed.

## SmartArt direct text opaque characters — ADV-02.1.2.1

The source graph now reports direct non-XML-whitespace characters inside text bodies, paragraphs, runs, fields and breaks as uninterpreted structure instead of silently discarding them. It omits the affected text scope, retaining other independently read content and source locations; a graph with this finding is partial. The optional source/cache comparison also rejects non-XML whitespace outside text leaves instead of treating characters such as NBSP as formatting indentation. Both dialects, XML indentation, source and cache boundaries, and browser ESM/global parity are covered. This does not admit graph editing, cache freshness or full rich-text interpretation; the task and its dependencies remain open.
